TL;DR: 本文研究利用GPT-4o生成目标端释义变体进行数据增强，以提升手语翻译（SLT）性能。采用两阶段训练：先在大规模增强语料上预训练，再在原始参考句上微调。在三个数据集上评估，PHOENIX14T的BLEU-4从9.56提升至10.33，但在接近饱和的GSL和极度稀疏的LSA-T数据集上效果有限。首次将LLM生成目标端释义和LLM-as-a-Judge评估应用于SLT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语翻译面临两个核心限制：配对的手语-文本语料库规模有限，以及目标词汇存在严重的长尾分布（高频词有限，低频词极多）。现有方法难以有效缓解数据稀疏性问题，因此探索利用大语言模型进行目标端数据增强。

Method: 使用GPT-4o对参考句生成受控的释义变体（保持手语输入不变），构建增强语料；采用Signformer风格的基于姿态的Transformer模型，实施两阶段训练策略：先在增强语料上预训练，再在原始参考句上微调。在三个具有不同挑战的数据集上进行评估。

Result: PHOENIX14T上BLEU-4从9.56提升至10.33；在GSL（希腊手语，接近饱和）和LSA-T（阿根廷手语，极端稀疏）数据集上效果有限，揭示了方法的局限性。

Conclusion: LLM生成的目标端释义增强在词汇多样性适中的数据集上有效，但在饱和或极端稀疏场景下提升有限。语义评估（LLM-as-a-Judge）揭示的忠实度提升远超词汇重叠指标（如BLEU）所反映的程度，表明传统指标可能低估了增强效果。

Abstract: Sign language translation (SLT) remains constrained by limited paired sign-video/text corpora and heavy-tailed target vocabularies. We study target-side augmentation in which GPT-4o generates controlled paraphrase variants of reference sentences while the sign input remains unchanged. A Signformer-style pose-based Transformer is trained under a two-stage schedule: pre-training on the augmented corpus followed by fine-tuning on the original references.
  We evaluate on three datasets spanning complementary challenges: PHOENIX14T (German Sign Language), with moderate lexical diversity; GSL (Greek Sign Language), with highly ontrolled, repetitive recordings; and LSA-T (Argentinian Sign Language), with severe long-tail sparsity. On PHOENIX14T, augmentation improves BLEU-4 from 9.56 to 10.33. The near-saturated GSL baseline and extremely sparse LSA-T setting reveal the limits of the approach. To our knowledge, this is the first study to apply LLM-generated target-side araphrases and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation to SLT. The semantic evaluation reveals gains in fidelity that lexical overlap metrics understate.

</details>


### [77] ["Intelegi Româneşte?'' A Recipe for Romanian Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31401)
*Mihai Masala,Marius Leordeanu,Mihai Dascalu,Traian Rebedea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了构建罗马尼亚语视觉-语言模型（VLM）的全流程，包括数据构建（机器翻译英文语料）和架构选择（视觉骨干、语言骨干、OCR数据），并提出了本土文化评估集HoraVQA。实验表明，罗马尼亚语适配的VLM持续优于同尺寸模型，甚至超越更大尺寸模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM主要针对英语开发，在低资源语言（如罗马尼亚语）上表现急剧下降，且缺乏大规模图像-文本语料库和文化相关的评估基准。

Method: (1) 将英文VLM训练和评估语料通过机器翻译（文本注释和图像内文本）转为罗马尼亚语，保持视觉基础；(2) 消融实验分离不同视觉骨干、语言骨干（多语言vs.罗马尼亚语适配LLM）和OCR数据的贡献；(3) 构建HoraVQA本土文化评估集。

Result: 罗马尼亚语适配的VLM持续优于同尺寸模型，且在所有评估基准上甚至超越下一个更大尺寸类别的模型。

Conclusion: 通过语言适配（尤其是罗马尼亚语优化的LLM骨干）和机器翻译数据，可在低资源语言上构建高性能VLM，超越单纯扩大模型规模的效果。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) largely follow the text-only LLM trajectory, excelling on English benchmarks but sharply degrading on low-resource languages, where neither large-scale image-text corpora nor culturally grounded evaluations exist. We present a systematic study of building a language-specific VLM for Romanian, covering the full pipeline from data construction to architectural choices. We translate established English VLM training and evaluation corpora into Romanian, applying machine translation to textual annotations and to in-image text, preserving visual grounding while adapting the textual content. Using this data, we train and ablate a series of VLMs to isolate the contribution of (i) vision backbones of varying scale and pretraining, (ii) language backbones from multilingual to Romanian-adapted LLMs, and (iii) OCR-style image-text data. We further curate HoraVQA, a culturally native evaluation set grounded in Romanian everyday scenes. Romanian-adapted VLMs consistently outperform their same-sized counterparts and, across all evaluated benchmarks, even surpass models from the next larger size category.

</details>


### [78] [The Sword, Shield, and Achilles' Heel: Characterizing the Linguistic Inductive Bias of Large Language Models for Spatial Reasoning in Navigation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31404)
*Xudong Zhang,Jian Yang,Shengkai Wang,Jiangpeng Tian,Shaowen Chen,Xian Wei,Ke Li,Xiong You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于LLM的导航系统中，文本化空间表示的**语言结构**和**上下文特征**如何影响LLM的导航规划行为。通过双干预框架（表示干预+上下文干预），发现：拓扑信息是稳健规划的支柱；语言格式因模型大小、任务需求和压缩程度而异，是一把双刃剑；语义信息是致命弱点——错误语义会系统性破坏规划过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM导航系统通常构建显式空间表示（拓扑图、语义栅格图）并将其转为文本描述输入LLM，但**文本表示的语言结构**和**上下文特征选择**被视为中性工程决策，而非影响LLM行为的关键因素。本文旨在填补这一认知空白。

Method: 提出**双干预框架**：1）**表示干预**：改变语言格式和语言压缩程度；2）**上下文干预**：结合上下文特征组合与冲突探测，明确LLM处理不同上下文线索时的偏好与弱点。在多种空间推理任务和多模型规模上进行实验。

Result: 1）拓扑信息是稳健规划的"坚固盾牌"和骨干；2）语言格式是"双刃剑"，效果取决于模型大小、任务需求和压缩程度；3）语义信息是"致命弱点"，错误语义线索会系统性破坏规划过程。

Conclusion: 有效基于LLM的导航文本表示应：保持拓扑完整性、根据模型容量校准表示压缩程度、确保语义正确性，而非简单采用单一表示形式。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based navigation systems commonly construct explicit spatial representations (e.g., topological graphs, semantic raster maps) and translate them into textual descriptions as LLMs' inputs. However, the linguistic structures of such text-based spatial representations and the choices of contextual features (e.g., topology, geometry) they contain are often treated as neutral engineering decisions rather than key factors that shape LLMs' behavior. To fill the gap, we propose a dual-interventional framework that disentangles linguistic structures from different contextual cues to evaluate the linguistic inductive bias of LLMs for navigation planning. In the framework, representation intervention varies the linguistic format and the degree of linguistic compression, clarifying when linguistic representations support or inhibit navigation planning. Context intervention, combined with contextual feature combination and conflict probing, explicitly clarifies the preferences and weaknesses of LLMs when processing different contextual cues. Experiments across diverse spatial reasoning tasks and multiple model scales reveal a consistent pattern: topological information is a sturdy shield and the backbone of robust planning; linguistic format is a double-edged sword whose effect depends on model size, task demands, and the compression level; and semantic information is a fatal Achilles' heel -- incorrect semantic cues can systematically derail the planning process. Overall, our study shows that effective text-based spatial representations in LLM-based navigation should preserve topological integrity, calibrate representational compression to model capacity, and ensure semantic correctness, rather than simply adopting a single representation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jonesdong150/LLM-Navigation-Inductive-Bias.

</details>


### [79] [SCOPE: Self-Play via Co-Evolving Policies for Open-Ended Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31433)
*Wai-Chung Kwan,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCOPE是一种无需外部数据的自博弈框架，通过协同进化Challenger和Solver两个策略，使语言模型在开放任务上自我提升。在三个7-8B模型上，SCOPE在8个开放任务基准上最高提升+10.4分，在7个保留的短问答基准上最高提升+13.8分，超越了使用约9K curated prompts训练的GRPO_data。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自博弈方法依赖规则可验证的答案或人工/前沿模型裁判，无法有效处理开放式任务。作者希望设计一种无需外部数据、不依赖预定义答案的自博弈框架，使语言模型能在开放式任务中自我进化。

Method: SCOPE框架包含两个协同进化的策略：(1) Challenger从文档中生成任务；(2) Solver通过多轮检索回答这些任务。使用初始模型的冻结副本作为自我裁判，该裁判根据源文档编写任务特定的评分标准，并据此对Solver的回复进行评分。

Result: 在三个7-8B指令微调模型上，SCOPE在8个开放任务基准上最高提升+10.4分，在7个保留的短问答基准上最高提升+13.8分，性能匹配或超过使用约9K curated prompts训练的GRPO_data。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明：(1) Challenger的协同进化对保持任务处于Solver前沿是必要的；(2) 性能提升来自检索和合成两方面改进，相对贡献因任务而异；(3) 评分标准生成质量是自我裁判的瓶颈。

Abstract: Self-play can train language models without external supervision. However, existing methods require rule-checkable answers, leaving open-ended tasks dependent on curated prompts or frontier-model judges. We introduce SCOPE, a data-free self-play framework for open-ended tasks that co-evolves two policies: a Challenger that generates document-grounded tasks, and a Solver that answers them through multi-turn retrieval. A frozen copy of the initial model serves as the self-judge, which writes task-specific rubrics from the source document and grades Solver responses against them. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models (Qwen2.5, Qwen3, OLMo-3), SCOPE improves open-ended performance by up to +10.4 points on eight benchmarks and matches or exceeds GRPO_data trained on ~9K curated prompts. Although trained only on open-ended tasks, SCOPE also improves held-out short-form QA by up to +13.8 points on seven held-out benchmarks, surpassing GRPO_data on all three models. Ablations show that co-evolving the Challenger is necessary to keep tasks near the Solver's frontier, that gains arise from improvements in both retrieval and synthesis with the relative contribution varying by task, and that rubric generation quality is the bottleneck for self-judging.

</details>


### [80] [Fine-grained Verification via Diagnostic Reasoning Supervision for Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31446)
*Wenna Lai,Haoran Xie,Guandong Xu,Qing Li,S. Joe Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FiVeD框架，通过细粒度验证与诊断推理监督来提升ASTE任务中三元组提取的可靠性，在多个基线上最高提升3.53 F1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASTE工作主要关注端到端提取，而提取后验证（post hoc verification）未被充分探索，导致预测的三元组可能局部合理但全局无效，限制了系统的可靠性。

Method: 提出FiVeD框架，训练验证器时结合多个互补目标：有效性分类与质量评分作为主要任务，错误类型分类与理由生成作为辅助任务。定义分层错误类别并在语义与句法约束下构造 plausible 错误三元组，利用LLM结合任务特定评分规则生成质量分数与诊断理由。

Result: 在多个ASTE基线上，FiVeD作为即插即用的验证模块，一致地提升了提取性能，最高提升3.53 F1点。

Conclusion: 细粒度的验证机制能够有效过滤或重排不同提取器的输出结果，支持可调节的精确率-召回率权衡，提升了ASTE系统的可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: Aspect Sentiment Triplet Extraction (ASTE) aims to identify aspect terms, opinion terms, and sentiment polarities as structured triplets, providing essential inputs for downstream information system applications such as opinion mining, explainable recommendations, and review summarization. Prior work mainly focuses on end-to-end extraction, while post hoc verification of extracted triplets remains comparatively underexplored. This gap limits the reliability of ASTE systems, since predicted triplets may be locally plausible while being globally invalid. Moreover, candidate invalidity is multi-faceted and candidate usability is inherently graded, motivating a fine-grained verification mechanism that can filter or re-rank outputs from diverse extractors. In this paper, we propose FiVeD, a framework for Fine-grained Verification with Diagnostic reasoning supervision. Specifically, the verifier is trained with multiple complementary objectives, including validity classification and quality score estimation as primary tasks, with error type classification and rationale generation as auxiliary tasks. We define hierarchical error categories and construct plausible incorrect triplets under semantic and syntactic constraints, and leverage an off-the-shelf LLM with task-specific rubrics to produce quality scores and diagnostic rationales. During inference, the resulting quality scores are used to filter candidate outputs, supporting adjustable precision-recall tradeoffs. Experiments across multiple ASTE baselines demonstrate that FiVeD consistently improves extraction performance by up to 3.53 F1 points as a plug-and-play verification module.

</details>


### [81] [Translation Analytics for Freelancers II: Benchmarking Local LLMs for Confidential Translation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31452)
*Yuri Balashov,Rex VanHorn,Mingxi Xu,Austin Downes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对隐私敏感领域（禁止使用云引擎和商业LLM）的离线翻译需求，在前期工作基础上扩展了三语语料库（RFTC）为多语语料库（RFMC），新增德语和简体中文对齐翻译。通过Ollama在四个语言方向上对多个本地可运行的语言模型进行基准测试（1000+句子），与商业NMT（DeepL、百度）、前沿LLM（GPT-5.2）及专业级本地NMT系统对比。结果表明，精心选择的本地LLM在翻译质量上可匹配或超越本地NMT系统和前沿LLM，但仍落后于顶级商业NMT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自由译者和小型语言服务提供商提供实用、低门槛的方法来评估翻译技术，特别针对隐私约束下无法使用云端引擎和商业LLM的机密敏感领域，解决高风险的离线翻译需求。

Method: 1) 将Reeve Foundation Trilingual Corpus (RFTC)扩展为多语语料库(RFMC)，新增德语和简体中文语料；2) 使用Ollama在四个语言方向上对多个本地LLM进行基准测试(1000+句子)；3) 采用一致的单次提示调用，无微调或领域适配；4) 对比商业NMT(DeepL、百度)、前沿LLM(GPT-5.2)和专业级本地NMT系统(OPUS-CAT、NeuralDesktop、Promt)；5) 使用MATEO进行自动评估。

Result: 本地LLM在不同语言方向和模型规模上表现出显著差异；最佳的本地LLM可匹配或超越本地NMT系统和前沿LLM，但仍落后于顶级商业NMT系统。

Conclusion: 精心选择的本地LLM翻译对于隐私受限的专业人员具有可行性，为未来关于模型规模和多语言能力的研究提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Building on our previous work, this paper develops practical, low-barrier methods for freelance translators and smaller language service providers to evaluate translation technologies using rigorous yet accessible analytic methods. Here we address a high-stakes, specialized need: offline translation for confidentiality-sensitive domains in which privacy constraints preclude the use of cloud-based engines and commercial LLMs. We expand the Reeve Foundation Trilingual Corpus (RFTC) used in our previous work into a multilingual corpus (RFMC) by adding sentence-aligned German and Simplified Chinese reference translations. We then benchmark several locally runnable language models (via Ollama) across four language directions on 1000+ sentences selected from this corpus. We use consistent single-prompt calls without fine-tuning or domain adaptation, comparing local LLM outputs against commercial NMTs (DeepL, Baidu), a frontier LLM (GPT-5.2), and professional-grade local NMT systems (OPUS-CAT, NeuralDesktop, Promt). Automatic evaluation is conducted with MATEO. Results reveal substantial variation in local LLM performance across language directions and model sizes. The best local LLMs match or surpass local NMT systems and a frontier LLM, though they remain behind top commercial NMTs. These findings underscore the viability of carefully selected local LLM translation for privacy-constrained professionals and inform future research on model scaling and multilingual capability.

</details>


### [82] [Scaling Conversational Hungarian ASR: The BEA-Dialogue+ Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31469)
*Máté Gedeon,Piroska Zsófia Barta,Péter Mihajlik,Katalin Mády*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍BEA-Dialogue+语料库，将匈牙利语对话式ASR训练数据从85小时扩展至200小时，通过放宽说话人分割标准实现。实验表明，SOT微调在更大语料库上带来一致的词错误率改善，但未微调模型面临更大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匈牙利语对话式ASR受限于公开对话风格训练数据稀缺，现有BEA-Dialogue语料库因严格说话人不重叠划分仅有85小时可用数据，需扩展以支持更充分的模型训练。

Method: 通过放宽语料库划分标准（允许实验者和对话伙伴跨分割重叠，但保持主要说话人完全分离），将BEA-Dialogue扩展至200小时。采用Whisper和FastConformer模型，结合Serialized Output Training (SOT)微调进行对话转录。

Result: 更大语料库对未微调模型更具挑战性；SOT微调在WER、CER、cpWER和cpCER上均带来一致改善。

Conclusion: BEA-Dialogue+提供了更大且仍具挑战性的匈牙利语对话ASR基准，是训练和评估对话转录系统的实用资源。

Abstract: Conversational automatic speech recognition in Hungarian is constrained by the limited amount of publicly available dialogue-style training data. The BEA-Dialogue corpus addresses this need, but its strictly speaker-disjoint train/dev/eval split reduces the usable material to only 85 hours. In this paper, we introduce BEA-Dialogue+, an expanded version of the corpus that relaxes the split criterion for experimenters and dialogue partners while preserving complete separation of the primary speakers. This results in 200 hours of transcribed natural conversations and enables a controlled study of the trade-off between additional training data and speaker overlap across the splits. We evaluate several Whisper- and FastConformer-based models on both corpus versions, including Serialized Output Training (SOT)-based fine-tuning for dialogue transcription. Our results show that the larger corpus is more challenging for models without fine-tuning, whereas SOT-based adaptation yields consistent improvements in WER, CER, cpWER, and cpCER. Overall, BEA-Dialogue+ provides a substantially larger yet still demanding benchmark for Hungarian dialogue ASR, and a practical resource for training and evaluating dialogue transcription systems.

</details>


### [83] [Language Models Can Resolve Reference Compositionally, But It's Not Their Native Strength: The Case of the Personal Relation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31480)
*Bart Evelo,Meaghan Fowlie,Denis Paperno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过"个人关系任务"评估了LLM和人类在语义解释中的外延任务（指称对象）和内涵任务（结构化意义表征）上的表现，发现人类在外延任务上更优，而LLM在内涵任务上更优，表明缺乏指称基础是LLM模拟人类语言理解的关键缺失成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经模型（如LLM）是否真正获得了自然语言理解的组合能力，特别是区分语义解释中"外延"（指称世界中的对象）和"内涵"（结构化意义表征）两种互补任务。

Method: 在个人关系任务框架下，给定人物关系宇宙和复合名词短语（如"Amber's parent's friend"），分别评估人类和LLM在外延任务（找出所指的人）和内涵任务（生成公式如"friend(parent(amber))"）上的表现。

Result: 人类和LLM展现出相反的优势：人类在外延任务上表现更好，而LLM在内涵任务上表现更好。

Conclusion: LLM训练中缺乏指称基础（referential grounding）是模拟人类式语言理解的关键缺失环节；该研究为理解现代机器学习模型中的组合能力提供了更细致的视角。

Abstract: Do neural models, such as Large Language Models, genuinely acquire compositional abilities for interpretation of natural language? When we talk about semantic interpretation, we can distinguish two complementary aspects: establishing what an expression refers to in the world (which we call the Extensional task) and representing its sense in a structured way (which we call the Intensional task). We evaluate LLMs and humans on both tasks in the setting of the Personal Relation Task (Paperno 2022) in which, given a universe of people and their relationships with each other, one is asked to interpret a noun phrase such as "Amber's parent's friend". Here, for the Intensional task, the answer is the formula "friend(parent(amber))", and for the Extensional task, the person. We find that humans and LLMs show opposite strengths: humans perform better on Extensional than Intensional tasks, and LLMs vice versa. Our methodology brings greater nuance to the understanding of compositional abilities in modern machine learning models. Our results support the notion that the lack of referential grounding in LLM training is a crucial missing component in mimicking human-like language understanding.

</details>


### [84] [BenHalluEval: A Multi-Task Hallucination Evaluation Framework for Large Language Models on Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31483)
*Shefayat E Shams Adib,Ahmed Alfey Sani,Ekramul Alam Esham,Ajwad Abrar,Ishmam Tashdeed,Md Taukir Azam Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了BenHalluEval，首个针对孟加拉语大模型幻觉的细粒度评估框架，涵盖四类任务、12种幻觉类型，并设计了双轨校准指标BenHalluScore。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语是全球第六大语言，但缺乏对大语言模型幻觉的系统评估，现有研究主要聚焦高资源语言。

Method: 构建12,000个幻觉候选样本（GPT-5.4生成），覆盖GQA、代码混合QA、摘要和推理四任务及12种幻觉类型；提出双轨评估协议（Track A测量假阳性率，Track B测量幻觉检测率）及BenHalluScore校准指标。

Result: BenHalluScore范围7.72%-55.42%，模型和任务间差异显著；思维链提示虽改变响应分布，但未持续改善幻觉判别能力。

Conclusion: BenHalluEval建立了孟加拉语首个专用幻觉基准，揭示单轨评估和仅提示策略对低资源语言的不足。

Abstract: Despite Bengali being the sixth most spoken language in the world, no prior work has systematically evaluated hallucination in large language models (LLMs) for Bengali. We introduce BenHalluEval, a fine-grained hallucination evaluation framework for Bengali covering four tasks: Generative Question Answering (GQA), Bangla-English Code-Mixed QA, Summarization, and Reasoning. We construct 12,000 hallucinated candidates using GPT-5.4 across twelve task-specific hallucination types, drawn from three existing Bengali datasets, and evaluate seven LLMs spanning reasoning-oriented, multilingual, and Bengali-centric categories under a dual-track protocol that independently measures false-positive rate on ground-truth instances (Track A) and hallucination detection rate on hallucinated candidates (Track B). To jointly penalise both failure modes and prevent inflated scores from uniform response bias, we propose BenHalluScore, a dual-track calibration metric that ranges from 7.72% to 55.42% across models and tasks, revealing substantial variation in hallucination calibration. Chain-of-thought prompting, applied as a mitigation strategy, shifts response distributions without consistently improving hallucination discrimination. BenHalluEval establishes the first dedicated hallucination benchmark for Bengali and highlights the inadequacy of single-track and prompting-only evaluation approaches for low-resource language settings. The dataset and code are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/BanglaHalluEval-EB77.

</details>


### [85] [Are Full Rollouts Necessary for On-Policy Distillation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31490)
*Yaocheng Zhang,Jiajun Chai,Songjun Tu,Yuqian Fu,Xiaohan Wang,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin,Qichao Zhang,Yuanheng Zhu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种控制 rollout 长度的策略（渐进式扩展的 POPD 和固定截断的 TOPD），在不牺牲性能的前提下显著提升同策略蒸馏（OPD）的训练效率，数学推理实验中最高提速3倍，TOPD仅需10%的rollout长度即可达到原性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OPD在训练时生成完整rollout，计算成本高，且早期训练中后期位置的学生轨迹可能收到不可靠的教师反馈。作者识别出rollout长度是OPD效率瓶颈，并指出OPD不像RLVR那样需要完整轨迹或最终答案奖励来提供学习信号，因此完整rollout并非必要。

Method: 提出两种rollout长度控制策略：渐进式OPD（POPD）在训练过程中逐步扩展rollout长度；截断式OPD（TOPD）在固定截断长度上永久执行蒸馏，仅保留可靠的前段轨迹。

Result: 数学推理实验中，POPD将OPD训练效率提升最高3倍；TOPD仅用10%的rollout长度即可达到与完整OPD相当的性能，显著减少了端到端时间和内存占用。

Conclusion: 控制rollout长度是实现更高效同策略蒸馏的简单且实用的途径。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher feedback along rollouts generated by the student and has emerged as a promising post-training paradigm for long-horizon reasoning. However, standard OPD typically generates full rollouts during training, which is computationally expensive and may expose the student to unreliable teacher feedback at late rollout positions, especially during early training. We identify the rollout horizon as a key bottleneck in OPD that substantially impacts training efficiency. Unlike Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), OPD does not require a complete trajectory or a final answer reward to provide learning signals. This observation suggests that full rollouts may not always be necessary for effective OPD. Motivated by this insight, we propose two simple horizon-control strategies: Progressive OPD (POPD), which gradually expands the rollout horizon during training, and Truncated OPD (TOPD), which permanently performs distillation on reliable truncated rollouts. Experiments on mathematical reasoning show that POPD improves the training efficiency of OPD by up to 3$\times$, while TOPD matches OPD performance using only 10\% of the rollout horizon, leading to substantial wall-clock and memory reductions. These results demonstrate that controlling the rollout horizon offers a simple and practical path to more efficient OPD.

</details>


### [86] [Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31494)
*Zheyu Zhang,Shuo Yang,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRP，一种无需梯度的算子，通过将奖励加权聚合、兼容性重加权和验证集门控相结合，把扰动后的奖励最优专家种群折叠成单个可部署模型，从而替代推理时集成。在5个模型（0.5B-8B）和5个任务上，CoRP平均提升8.1分，使用RandOpt十分之一的扰动预算，单次推理超越RandOpt 6.5分，并恢复50次多数投票集成一半以上的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RandOpt等方法通过采样高斯扰动并集成Top-K奖励专家，虽然与PPO/GRPO竞争力相当，但推理时需K次前向传播且不适用于自由生成。作者希望将奖励种群折叠成单个可部署模型，用一次一致性更新替代推理时集成。

Method: 通过25个模型-任务对的拆分半分析发现每种情况都存在可重复的低秩结构。基于此设计CoRP算子：(1) 奖励加权聚合，(2) 兼容性重加权，(3) 验证集门控。全程无需语言模型梯度。

Result: 在5个语言模型（0.5B-8B）和数学、代码、创意写作5个任务上，CoRP平均提升8.1分。使用RandOpt十分之一的扰动预算，CoRP单次推理超越RandOpt 6.5分，恢复50次多数投票集成一半以上增益。

Conclusion: CoRP证明了通过无梯度算子将奖励最优种群折叠成单一模型的有效性，在显著降低推理成本的同时保持了强性能。

Abstract: Post-training of language models is commonly framed as a sample-score-update loop implemented by gradient descent. A recent line of work, exemplified by RandOpt, relocates this loop to weight space, sampling Gaussian perturbations around a pretrained model and ensembling the top-K rewarded specialists at inference. While competitive with PPO and GRPO under matched training compute, this prediction-level ensemble incurs K forward passes per test example and does not extend cleanly to free-form generation. We ask whether the rewarded population can instead be folded into a single deployable model, replacing the inference-time ensemble with one consolidated update. A split-half analysis over 25 model-task pairs reveals reproducible low-rank structure in every case. We turn this geometry into CoRP (Consolidating Rewarded Perturbations), a gradient-free operator that combines reward-weighted aggregation, compatibility-aware reweighting, and a held-out validation gate, with no gradient flowing through the language model. Across five language models from 0.5B to 8B and five tasks covering math, code, and creative writing, CoRP improves the base model by 8.1 points on average. Using one tenth of RandOpt's perturbation budget, CoRP exceeds single-inference RandOpt by 6.5 points and recovers more than half of the gain of the 50-pass majority-vote ensemble, at one forward pass per test example.

</details>


### [87] [Reliable Multilingual Orthopedic Decision Support from Clinical Narratives: Language-Aware Adaptation and Verification-Guided Deferral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31512)
*Danish Ali,Li Xiaojian,Sundas Iqbal,Farrukh Zaidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向可靠性评估的多语言骨科临床决策支持框架，通过领域自适应编码器IndicBERT-HPA（融合语言感知骨科适配器头）在英语、印地语和旁遮普语上对骨科自由文本进行分类。在自然临床分布下，IndicBERT-HPA取得平均Macro-F1 0.8792、Macro-AUROC 0.894和AUPRC 0.902的最佳性能。该框架还包含确定性的选择性验证层（置信度门控、证据一致性检查、语言风险筛查），在72.3%覆盖率下达到84.4%的选择性准确率和0.76的选择性Macro-F1，优于全接受预测的71.5%准确率和0.65 Macro-F1，支持明确的决策延迟机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源医疗场景中，多语言骨科决策支持面临专业术语、混合文字、证据不完整、标签不平衡和语言依赖性文档模式等挑战，现有方法在可靠性和跨语言稳定性上不足。

Method: 对比任务对齐的多语言Transformer编码器、任务微调的DistilBERT基线、零样本指令微调LLM和领域自适应编码器IndicBERT-HPA（在IndicBERT上增加语言感知的骨科适配器头）。评估超越聚合准确率，涵盖每类性能、ROC-AUC、AUPRC、期望校准误差、跨语言稳定性和鲁棒性。额外实现确定性选择性验证层，结合置信度门控、证据一致性检查和语言风险筛查。

Result: 零样本LLM在封闭集分类上远不如任务自适应编码器，且存在语言依赖性不稳定。IndicBERT-HPA在自然临床分布下最优：Macro-F1 0.8792，Macro-AUROC 0.894，AUPRC 0.902。选择性验证层在72.3%覆盖率下实现84.4%准确率和0.76 Macro-F1，显著优于全接受基线。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的领域特定编码器（如IndicBERT-HPA）在多语言骨科封闭集分类中显著优于零样本LLM；结合确定性选择性验证的可靠性框架能有效实现明确的决策延迟，提升临床决策支持的可信度。

Abstract: Multilingual orthopedic decision support remains challenging in low-resource healthcare settings, where clinical narratives contain specialized terminology, mixed scripts, incomplete evidence, label imbalance and language-dependent documentation patterns. This article presents a reliability-oriented framework for classifying free-text orthopedic notes in English, Hindi and Punjabi. We compare task-aligned multilingual transformer encoders, a task-fine-tuned DistilBERT baseline, zero-shot instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) and a domain-adaptive encoder, IndicBERT-HPA. IndicBERT-HPA augments IndicBERT with language-aware orthopedic adapter heads to support clinically relevant multilingual representation learning. Evaluation extends beyond aggregate accuracy to per-class performance, ROC-AUC, AUPRC, expected calibration error, cross-language stability and robustness under controlled balanced and natural-prevalence distributions. The evaluated zero-shot LLMs remain substantially less effective than task-adapted encoders for closed-set classification, with language-dependent instability. Under natural clinical prevalence, IndicBERT-HPA achieves the strongest overall performance, reaching an averaged Macro-F1 of 0.8792, Macro-AUROC of 0.894 and AUPRC of 0.902. We further implement a deterministic selective-verification layer combining confidence gating, evidence-consistency checking and language-risk screening. On a randomly selected held-out 5,000-record subset, it achieves 84.4% selective accuracy and 0.76 selective Macro-F1 at 72.3% coverage, compared with 71.5% accuracy and 0.65 Macro-F1 for accept-all prediction. These results support reliability-oriented multilingual clinical decision support with explicit deferral.

</details>


### [88] [UniAudio-Token: Empowering Semantic Speech Tokenizers with General Audio Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31521)
*Yuhan Song,Linhao Zhang,Aiwei Liu,Chuhan Wu,Sijun Zhang,Wei Jia,Yuan Liu,Houfeng Wang,Xiao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniAudio-Token 是一个通用音频标记框架，通过语义-声学基元（SAP）和语义-声学均衡（SAE）两项关键创新，在保持语义标记器语言能力的同时，赋予其通用音频感知能力，在理解和生成任务上均优于现有单码本基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义语音标记器虽然具有紧凑的单码本设计和良好的语言对齐能力，但由于过度关注语言抽象导致"声学盲区"，难以适用于非语音的通用音频任务。作者旨在弥补这一信息损失，使语义标记器具备通用音频感知能力而不损害其语音能力。

Method: 提出UniAudio-Token框架，包含两项创新：(1) 语义-声学基元（SAP），将音频分解为语言内容、声音属性和听觉场景基元，提供结构化监督；(2) 语义-声学均衡（SAE），引入内容感知门控机制，从浅层自适应恢复细粒度声学细节。

Result: UniAudio-Token学习了全面的通用表示，同时保持高保真语音生成。与下游LLM集成时，在理解和生成任务上均优于所有单码本基线标记器，可作为统一的音频接口。

Conclusion: 通过SAP和SAE两个创新，有效解决了语义标记器的声学盲区问题，实现了通用音频感知能力，为Audio-LLM提供了一个统一的音频接口方案。

Abstract: Semantic speech tokenizers have become a widely used interface for Audio-LLMs, owing to their compact single-codebook design and strong linguistic alignment. However, their focus on linguistic abstraction induces acoustic blindness, limiting their applicability beyond speech-centric tasks. We propose UniAudio-Token, a framework that empowers semantic tokenizers with general audio perception without compromising speech ability. Instead of altering the semantic paradigm, UniAudio-Token mitigates its information loss through two key innovations: (1) Semantic-Acoustic Primitives (SAP) provide structured supervision by decomposing audio into linguistic content, vocal attributes, and auditory-scene primitives; and (2) Semantic-Acoustic Equilibrium (SAE) introduces a content-aware gating mechanism that adaptively restores fine-grained acoustic details from shallow layers. Extensive evaluations show that UniAudio-Token learns comprehensive universal representations while preserving high-fidelity speech generation. When integrated with downstream LLMs, it outperforms all single-codebook baseline tokenizers on both understanding and generation tasks, effectively serving as a unified audio interface. We publicly release all our code, including training and inference scripts, together with the model checkpoints at https://github.com/Tencent/Universal_Audio_Tokenizer.

</details>


### [89] [Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31545)
*Yilun Qiu,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Cilin Yan,Jiayin Cai,Xiaolong Jiang,Yao Hu,Yoko Yamakata,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PARL框架，将个性化评估形式化为学习问题，通过从用户历史中学习偏好感知评估准则，结合判别式强化学习目标，实现对用户特定偏好的高保真评估。实验表明PARL能可靠识别符合用户偏好的响应，并在用户和任务间泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法（自动指标、LLM-as-a-judge）无法捕捉长期交互历史中嵌入的主观、用户特定偏好，缺乏可靠且有效的个性化评估方法。

Method: 提出"个性化评估即学习"范式，核心框架PARL包含：(1) 从原始用户历史中学习偏好感知评估准则（rubric induction）；(2) 自验证机制确保与用户偏好一致；(3) 判别式强化学习目标，对比用户作者响应与竞争性个性化模型输出，捕捉精准的用户特定决策边界。

Result: 在真实个性化文本生成任务上，PARL持续诱导出高保真评估准则，可靠识别用户对齐的响应，并在用户和任务间泛化，同时捕捉稳定的风格偏好和细粒度评估模式。

Conclusion: 个性化评估应从静态判断转向学习问题，PARL通过偏好感知准则学习和自验证机制，有效解决了代表性、用户一致性和区分性三个核心原则。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve from general-purpose assistants to user-centric agents, personalization has become central to aligning model behavior with individual preferences, making the evaluation of personalized alignment a critical bottleneck. Existing evaluation methods-ranging from automatic metrics to LLM-as-a-judge approaches-fail to capture subjective, user-specific preferences embedded in long-term interaction histories. We identify three essential principles for reliable and effective personalized evaluation: Representativeness, User-Consistency, and Discriminativeness. To address these principles, we introduce Personalized Evaluation as Learning, a paradigm that formulates personalized evaluation as a learning problem rather than a static judgment. Under this paradigm, we propose PARL (Preference-Aware Rubric Learning for Personalized Evaluation), a framework that learns to induce preference-aware evaluation rubrics directly from raw user histories and performs a self-validation mechanism to ensure consistency with the user's preferences. PARL integrates rubric induction with a discriminative reinforcement learning objective that contrasts user-authored responses against competitive personalized model outputs, enabling the learned rubrics to capture precise, user-specific decision boundaries. Experiments on real-world personalized text generation tasks show that PARL consistently induces high-fidelity rubrics that reliably identify user-aligned responses and generalize across users and tasks, while capturing stable stylistic preferences and fine-grained evaluative patterns. To ensure reproducibility, our code is available at https://github.com/SnowCharmQ/PARL.

</details>


### [90] [Semantic Triplet Restoration: A Novel Protocol for Hierarchical Table Understanding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31550)
*Yibin Zhao,Fangxin Shang,Dingrui Yang,Yuqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出语义三元组重建（STR）协议，将表格单元重写为<条目路径, 特征路径, 值>形式的原子事实，并设计TripletQL查询感知路由器，在多个表格问答基准上匹配或超越HTML基线且减少输入token，对小模型和长表格场景尤其有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格问答方法将表格转为HTML或Markdown等面向布局的序列化格式，这些格式引入标记开销，且需要大语言模型从行列跨度推断表头与单元格的对齐关系，导致语义关系隐式编码、推理效率低下。

Method: 提出语义三元组重建（STR）协议，将每个单元格表示为原子事实<条目路径, 特征路径, 值>，其中条目路径指定行实体、特征路径指定层级属性、值为单元格内容；并设计轻量级查询感知路由器TripletQL，基于STR为每个问题选择合适的三元组渲染子集。

Result: 在四个中英文表格问答基准上，STR匹配或超越基于HTML的基线方法，同时减少输入token数；对小语言模型和长表格场景，相对收益更大。

Conclusion: 显式语义表示在有限推理预算下特别有用，验证了STR协议作为表格问答中高效替代方案的可行性。

Abstract: Table question answering requires models to recover semantic relations encoded implicitly by two-dimensional layout, merged cells, and hierarchical headers. Current pipelines typically use HTML or Markdown as intermediate table representations, but these layout-oriented serializations introduce markup overhead and require large language models to infer header-cell alignments from row and column spans. We propose Semantic Triplet Restoration (STR), a protocol that rewrites each cell as an atomic fact <item path, feature path, value>, where the item path specifies the row-wise entity, the feature path specifies the hierarchical attribute, and the value contains the cell content. We also present TripletQL, a lightweight query-aware router that uses STR to select an appropriate rendering or filtered subset of triplets for each question. Across four Chinese and English table-QA benchmarks, STR matches or improves upon HTML-based baselines while reducing input tokens. The relative benefit grows for smaller language models and longer table contexts, suggesting that explicit semantic representations are especially useful under constrained inference budgets. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Phoenix-ni/STR.git .

</details>


### [91] [What Am I Missing? Question-Answering as Hidden State Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31561)
*Chu Fei Luo,Samuel Dahan,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用提问作为推理时干预手段，通过学生-教师框架中探针分析隐藏状态来预测轨迹正确性，并将提问建模为序列决策问题，发现提问的成功依赖于模型的自一致性，且在诊断与纠正之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管链式推理已被广泛使用，但LLM推理过程的内部机制仍不明确，同一输入或部分解多次采样可能产生不同答案，亟需理解并干预这一过程。

Method: 提出学生-教师框架，学生在提问前后隐藏状态上训练探针，预测轨迹最终正确性；将提问建模为序列决策问题，利用探针作为质量分数定义门控策略，选择最大化正确可能性的问题进行干预。

Result: 探针能在教师生成答案前有效预测轨迹正确性，表明提问过程中的自我诊断信号比信息传递更重要；但门控策略发现提问干预对正确轨迹的伤害与对错误轨迹的修复同样常见，检测与恢复之间存在差距。

Conclusion: 提问作为干预手段的效果高度依赖模型的自一致性；诊断与纠正之间的差距揭示了语言模型在不确定性下进行自我精炼能力的局限性。

Abstract: Test-time reasoning has become a significant field of study since the introduction of chain-of-thought reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, the mechanisms of this reasoning process are still under-explored -- from the same input prompt, and even the same partial solution, LLMs can produce varied answers if sampled multiple times. We propose to leverage question-asking as an inference-time intervention that articulates information about the model's hidden state. To achieve that, we present a student-teacher setting where a student asks questions to a teacher. We train a probe on the student's hidden state before and after asking a question and find it is predictive of the trajectory's final correctness, even before generating the teacher's answer. This suggests there is a meaningful signal from the self-diagnosis that occurs during question generation rather than information transfer from the teacher. We then frame question-asking as a sequential decision problem, using this probe as a quality score, and define a gating policy to ask questions that maximize likelihood of correctness. We find that the success of question-asking as an intervention is largely dependent on the model's self-consistency. Our empirical results show a gap between detection and recovery; while our gating policy captures model correctness and uncertainty, interventions are equally likely to harm correct trajectories as they are to recover incorrect ones. This gap between diagnosis and correction has broader implications on language models' capacity for self-refinement under uncertainty.

</details>


### [92] [Disagreeing Rationales: Rethinking Classification and Explainability Evaluation in Hate Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31563)
*Benedetta Muscato,Beiduo Chen,Gizem Gezici,Barbara Plank,Fosca Giannotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了主观NLP任务（如仇恨言论检测）中标注不一致和解释多样性的问题，提出了统一的监督框架，通过重新实现多种模型、训练策略和评估指标，在分类和可解释性两个维度上评估不同标签和解释表示空间的效果。实验表明，软表示（soft representations）在硬指标和软指标上均表现更优，揭示了在主观NLP任务中重新思考评估方式的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注中的意见分歧已被广泛认知，但token级人类解释（rationales）的变异性仍未得到充分探索。同时，如何评估人类标签和解释——以及如何聚合解释（超越多数投票）——尚不明确。解释可能提供人类推理的丰富信息，这些信息在风格、价值观和解读上存在差异，尤其在仇恨言论检测等主观NLP任务中，因此需要系统性研究。

Method: 将多样化的模型、训练策略、损失函数和现有评估指标统一到一个协议下，系统性地重新实现它们，跨越不同的标签和解释表示空间。分类指标围绕两个关键属性组织（预测性和分布性），可解释性指标通过三个互补维度（合理性、忠实性、复杂性）组织。在该统一监督框架中，评估模型在分类和可解释性指标上的表现，以及指标对标签（硬/软）和解释表示空间（硬/中间/软）选择的敏感性。

Result: 硬指标和软指标都更偏好软表示（soft representations），表明软表示在捕捉变异性方面更有效，这提示需要在主观NLP任务中重新思考评估方式。

Conclusion: 软表示在捕捉人类解释的多样性方面具有优势，现有的评估体系需要重构以更好地适应主观NLP任务的特点。

Abstract: Human disagreement is ubiquitous and well-known in labeling. However, variation in explanations, captured through token-level human rationales, remains far less explored. At the same time, it is unclear how to best evaluate human labels and rationales -- or even how to best aggregate rationales beyond majority vote -- in light of this variation. Yet, rationales may provide additional insights into the richness of human reasoning, that may differ in style, values and interpretations -- especially in subjective NLP tasks like hate speech detection. In this work, we unify diverse models, training strategies, loss functions, and existing evaluation metrics under a single protocol by systematically re-implementing them across different label and rationale representation spaces. Classification metrics are organized around two key properties -- predictive and distributional -- while explainability metrics through three complementary dimensions: plausibility, faithfulness, and complexity. In this unified supervision framework, we evaluate model behavior across classification and explainability metrics, as well as metric sensitivity to the choice of label (hard and soft) and rationale representation space (hard, intermediate and soft). Results show that both hard and soft metrics favor softer representations, highlighting their effectiveness in capturing variation and the need to rethink evaluation in subjective NLP.

</details>


### [93] [What Gets Unmasked First? Trajectory Analysis of Diffusion Models for Graph-to-Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31564)
*Qing Wang,Jacob Devasier,Chengkai Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究掩码扩散语言模型(MDLM)用于图到文本生成，发现MDLM优先生成实体，其次关系词和功能词，最后结构词。识别出SFT会破坏这一策略，提出λ缩放结构解码（无训练推理时修改）恢复+9.4 BLEU-4。提出Graph-LLaDA将图Transformer编码器集成到LLaDA解码中。跨数据集评估表明LLM和MDLM方法泛化性更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次系统研究掩码扩散语言模型在图到文本生成任务中的表现，分析其生成轨迹，并发现监督微调(SFT)引入的失败模式。

Method: 1) 分析MDLM生成轨迹中token去掩码的顺序模式；2) 提出λ缩放结构解码(lambda-scaled structural decoding)，一种无训练推理时修改方法，降低结构token置信度；3) 提出Graph-LLaDA，将图Transformer编码器集成到LLaDA解码过程中显式融入关系图结构。

Result: λ缩放结构解码恢复+9.4 BLEU-4；跨数据集评估表明LLM和MDLM方法比传统基线泛化性更好，传统基线过拟合数据集特定模式。

Conclusion: MDLM在图到文本生成中具有优越性，但SFT会破坏其固有的生成策略，所提出的方法有效缓解了该问题并提升了性能。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) for graph-to-text generation. We analyze MDLM generation trajectories -- the order in which tokens are unmasked during iterative decoding -- and find that, unlike autoregressive LLMs which generate text linearly, MDLMs naturally prioritize entities first, followed by relational and function words, with structural tokens resolved last. We further identify a previously undocumented failure mode of supervised fine-tuning: SFT disrupts this strategy by prematurely anchoring structural sentence-ending tokens early in the decoding trajectory, effectively fixing the output length which can lead to omitted or hallucinated information. To address this, we propose lambda-scaled structural decoding, a training-free inference-time modification that downweights structural token confidence and recovers +9.4 BLEU-4. Finally, we introduce Graph-LLaDA, which integrates a Graph Transformer encoder into LLaDA's decoding process to explicitly incorporate relational graph structure. Cross-dataset evaluation on LAGRANGE reveals that previous baselines overfit to dataset-specific patterns, while LLM- and MDLM-based approaches generalize significantly better.

</details>


### [94] [Language Models Learn Constructional Semantics, Not To Mention Syntax: Investigating LM Understanding of Paired-Focus Constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31586)
*Wesley Scivetti,Ethan Wilcox,Nathan Schneider,Kanishka Misra,Leonie Weissweiler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了开源大模型对英语中罕见配对焦点结构（如"let alone"、"much less"）的语义理解能力，发现中等规模模型能够掌握这些结构的语义，且该能力在训练后期出现，并与世界知识获取相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开源大模型是否具备对罕见构式（形式-意义配对）的鲁棒理解能力，以及这种知识习得背后的学习动态。

Method: 构建了针对英语配对焦点构式的新型数据集，利用标量形容词语义和世界常识测试模型理解能力；测试了多种参数规模、架构和预训练数据规模不同的模型；对开放检查点模型进行训练动态分析。

Result: 中等规模模型对配对焦点构式的形式和语义均敏感；人类规模数据训练的模型在所有语义评估中失败；配对焦点语义理解在训练后期出现，晚于句法知识习得，且与世界知识获取相关。

Conclusion: 中等规模开源模型能够掌握罕见配对焦点构式，证实了配对焦点构式知识与其他语义领域之间存在关联。

Abstract: Grasping the semantics of rare constructions (form-meaning pairings) has been shown to be a challenging problem that has currently only been solved by the largest LLMs. It remains an open question if open-source models have robust constructional understanding, and if so, what learning dynamics underlie the acquisition of this knowledge. Focusing on a set of rare Paired-Focus constructions in English (e.g. "let alone", "much less"), we construct a novel dataset to test their meanings using both scalar adjectival semantics and general world knowledge. Testing a wide range of models differing in parameter count, architecture, and pretraining dataset size, we find that several modestly sized models are sensitive to both the forms and the meanings of Paired-Focus constructions, though models trained on human-scale data fail at all meaning evaluations. Turning to training dynamics for a set of open-checkpoint models, we find that Paired-Focus understanding emerges later in training than Paired-Focus syntactic knowledge, and that learning of Paired-Focus semantics is correlated with gains in some domains of world knowledge. Overall, our empirical results support the conclusion that modestly sized open-source models can grasp the rare Paired-Focus constructions, and demonstrate a connection between knowledge of Paired-Focus constructions and other meaning domains.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Gait2Hip-60: A Unified Deep Learning Benchmark for Predicting Hip Muscle Forces and Joint Moments from Multi-Cadence Gait Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30374)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Ji Hou,Qing Sun,Xianzhi Gao,Bo Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种深度学习框架，直接从下肢步态运动学预测髋关节肌肉力和关节力矩，并在统一协议下比较了LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种序列模型。在60名健康成人数据上，Transformer表现最优（髋肌力R²=0.819，髋力矩R²=0.862）；在9名股骨头坏死患者的零样本外推测试中，Transformer仍保持中等预测能力（髋肌力R²=0.537，髋力矩R²=0.569），验证了从步态运动学估计髋关节动力学的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统依赖肌肉骨骼仿真估算髋关节肌肉力和关节力矩的方法虽然有效，但耗时且难以在临床场景中推广应用，亟需一种高效、可直接从步态运动学预测髋关节动力学参数的替代方法。

Method: 收集60名健康成人在三种节拍器引导步速下的步态数据，以10个双侧下肢关节角度为输入，OpenSim导出的髋肌力和髋关节力矩为输出，在统一数据划分、预处理和评估指标下，比较LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种深度学习模型，并将最优模型直接（零样本）测试于9名股骨头坏死患者。

Result: 健康受试者中Transformer最优：髋肌力预测RMSE=1.33 N/kg, MAE=0.57 N/kg, R²=0.819；髋力矩预测RMSE=0.11 Nm/kg, MAE=0.07 Nm/kg, R²=0.862。零样本外推至ONFH患者时，Transformer仍保持中等性能：髋肌力R²=0.537，髋力矩R²=0.569。

Conclusion: 从步态运动学直接估计髋关节动力学具有可行性，Transformer可作为强基线模型，但需在更广泛的病理数据上验证并提升泛化能力后方可应用于临床。

Abstract: Estimating hip muscle forces and joint moments during gait typically relies on musculoskeletal simulation, which is informative but time-consuming and difficult to apply in clinical settings. This study developed a deep learning framework to predict these hip dynamics parameters directly from lower-limb gait kinematics and compared three representative sequence models under a unified protocol. Gait data were collected from 60 healthy adults under three metronome-guided cadence conditions. Ten bilateral lower-limb joint angles were used as inputs, and OpenSim-derived hip muscle forces and hip joint moments were used as reference outputs. Three deep learning models of LSTM, Transformer, and Mamba were trained and evaluated using the same subject-level split, preprocessing pipeline, and metrics. The best model was then directly tested on an external cohort of 9 patients with osteonecrosis of the femoral head (ONFH) without retraining. In the healthy-subject benchmark, Transformer achieved the best subject-level mean performance for both hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.33 N/kg, MAE = 0.57 N/kg, R2 = 0.819) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.11 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.07 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.862), with similar advantages across walking cadences. In zero-shot external validation, Transformer retained moderate predictive ability in ONFH for hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.51 N/kg, MAE = 0.70 N/kg, R2 = 0.537) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.17 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.12 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.569). These findings support the feasibility of estimating hip dynamics from gait kinematics, identify Transformer as a strong baseline, and highlight the need for broader pathological validation and improved generalization before clinical application.

</details>


### [96] [When LLMs Learn to Be Consistently Wrong: A Multi-Model Study of Linear Representations of Synthetic Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30381)
*Vahideh Zolfaghari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过多模型范式（5种Transformer模型）研究合成性欺骗对齐的神经表征基础，发现基于LoRA微调的欺骗性模型在浅层（1-3层）即可通过线性探针实现近完美检测（AUC≥0.99），且该表征具有领域不变性和噪声鲁棒性，揭示了欺骗表征通过适度监督微调即可快速固化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺骗性对齐（Deceptive Alignment）是AI安全的核心挑战，但战略欺骗难以在受控环境中研究。本文采用合成性不诚实（Synthetic Dishonesty）——即通过直接优化不正确答案诱导的欺骗——作为可控实验平台，旨在探究学习性欺骗的神经表征基础及其可检测性。

Method: 采用多模型范式，对五种Transformer模型（Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B）使用LoRA进行诚实/欺骗变体的微调。训练线性/MLP探针检测基于平均池化隐藏状态的合成不诚实表征，并进行机制分析（Fisher判别比、有效秩、质心几何、方向稳定性、跨领域对齐、校准误差ECE）。

Result: 线性探针在四类架构的1-3层即达近完美AUC（≥0.99），Pythia-1.4B峰值0.705；Logistic回归探针一致匹配或超越MLP探针；TruthfulQA训练的探针泛化至MMLU（ΔAUC≈0）；Gemma-2模型在噪声鲁棒性上表现卓越；机制分析揭示Pythia/Llama/Qwen的表征坍缩与Gemma-2的高维保持两种模式；所有模型中欺骗方向在深层逐步巩固，1-4层可达最优校准（ECE<0.01，Pythia除外）。

Conclusion: 稳健、领域不变的不诚实表征可通过适度监督微调快速固化，这对基于激活的监控机制具有重要意义，同时支持线性表征假说（Linear Representation Hypothesis）。

Abstract: Deceptive alignment, in which models maintain accurate internal representations while deliberately producing false outputs, remains a central challenge in AI safety. While strategic deception is the primary long-term concern, synthetic dishonesty - induced via direct optimization on incorrect answers - provides a controlled testbed for studying the representational basis of learned deception. We introduce a multi-model paradigm in which honest and deceptive variants of five transformer models (Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B) are fine-tuned using LoRA on the same question distribution. Linear probes trained on mean-pooled hidden states detect synthetic dishonesty with near-perfect AUC (greater than or equal to 0.99) as early as layers 1-3 in four architectures, while Pythia-1.4B reaches a peak of 0.705. Logistic regression probes consistently match or outperform MLP probes, supporting the Linear Representation Hypothesis. Probes trained on TruthfulQA generalize with near-zero loss (Delta AUC approx. 0) to held-out MMLU subjects. Late-layer representations show strong robustness to Gaussian noise, with Gemma-2 models exhibiting exceptional stability. Mechanistic analysis of Fisher Discriminant Ratio, effective rank, centroid geometry, directional stability, cross-domain alignment, and calibration (ECE) reveals two regimes: representational collapse in Pythia/Llama/Qwen versus high-dimensional preservation in Gemma-2. Across all models, the dishonesty direction consolidates progressively in deeper layers, with optimal calibration (ECE less than 0.01 except Pythia) achievable in layers 1-4. These results demonstrate that robust, domain-invariant dishonesty representations can be rapidly entrenched via modest supervised fine-tuning, with implications for activation-based monitoring.

</details>


### [97] [A Novel Evaluation Metric for Unsupervised Learning in AIS-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection: MADQI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30388)
*Ismet Gocer,Zakirul Bhuiyan,Raza Hasan,Shakeel Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的系统框架用于检测海上自动识别系统（AIS）数据集中的异常，并引入了一种名为MADQI的无监督异常检测质量指标，该指标通过四个子指标（ARC、PPS、SDS、ECE）综合评估检测性能，实验在AIS数据集上取得了80.37%的MADQI得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无监督学习算法（如Isolation Forest）虽被广泛用于检测异常船只行为，但缺乏系统且有意义的评估指标，因此需要一种无需标注数据即可评估异常检测性能的方法。

Method: 提出MADQI复合指标，包含四个子指标：异常率一致性（ARC）、物理合理性得分（PPS）、得分分布分离度（SDS）和极端案例证据（ECE）；通过多块评估和自适应缩放进行自动归一化；利用Haversine距离计算分析AIS数据集的空间和行为特征。

Result: 在AIS数据集上MADQI得分为80.37%；ECE和ARC分别达到0.907和1.000，表明在检测极端异常和保持异常率一致性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该框架为无监督异常检测在海事AIS数据上的评估提供了可靠且有意义的方案，特别是在识别异常船只行为方面表现强劲。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new systematic framework for detecting anomalies in maritime Automatic Identification System (AIS) datasets. These anomalies include abnormal vessel behaviours related to speed, position jumps, time gaps, and turn angles. Although unsupervised learning algorithms such as Isolation Forest are widely used for detecting anomalous vessel movements, they often lack systematic and meaningful evaluation measures. To address this limitation, we propose a novel quality metric called Maritime Anomaly Detection Quality Index (MADQI). The prosed MADQI is a composite index designed to evaluate the anomaly detection performance of machine learning models without requiring labelled data. The proposed framework uses Haversine distance calculations to analyse AIS datasets and identify anomalies based on their spatial and behavioural characteristics. The proposed MADQI evaluation framework integrates four interconnected metrics: Anomaly Rate Consistency (ARC), Physical Plausibility Score (PPS), Score Distribution Separation (SDS), and Extreme Case Evidence (ECE). These metrics are combined through automatic normalisation using multi-chunk evaluation and adaptive scaling techniques. Experimental results on the AIS dataset show that the proposed framework achieved a MADQI score of 80.37%, demonstrating its effectiveness for unsupervised anomaly detection. In particular, the algorithm performed strongly in identifying abnormal vessel behaviour. Among the individual MADQI components, ECE and ARC achieved scores of 0.907 and 1.000, respectively, indicating excellent capability in detecting extreme anomalies and maintaining anomaly rate consistency. Overall, these results are encouraging and demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a reliable and meaningful approach for evaluating unsupervised anomaly detection in maritime AIS data.

</details>


### [98] [NumLeak: Public Numeric Benchmarks as Latent Labels in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30393)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NumLeak框架，系统性地测量了前沿大语言模型在公共数值基准测试中的记忆泄露问题。研究发现，顶级模型能够以极高精度（r=0.97-0.99）回忆金融因子、经济指标和气候数据，这种记忆能力导致模型在回答中产生虚假的相关性（如"日期→市场情绪"回归从r=0.74降至0.02）。通过黑盒API探测与白盒因果模型验证，论文揭示了记忆通道的存在，并提出一行系统提示防御可阻止99.8%的单轮后缀攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共数值基准测试可能出现在预训练数据中，因此基于日期的评估可能测量的是记忆性回忆而非样本外技能。现有黑盒探测方法可能低估了记忆泄露的程度，需要更系统的测量框架来量化这一现象。

Method: 提出NumLeak测量框架，结合API边界探测（生产模型）和白盒控制验证（开源因果语言模型）。通过多种子池化Pearson相关系数、解析率、logprob排名检测等指标，系统评估模型对各类数值基准的记忆程度，并设计残差化实验分离记忆效应。

Result: 顶级模型对Fama-French市场超额收益的回忆达到r=0.97-0.99，对其他因子、经济指标和气候数据也有类似精度。在最近发布的保留集上解析率降至21-57%，但回答的月份仍保持约0.99的相关性。白盒实验复现了剂量反应关系，logprob排名能检测到开放式生成遗漏的记忆。"日期→市场情绪"回归在残差化处理后从r=0.74降至r=0.02。一行系统提示防御可阻止99.8%的单轮后缀攻击。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM存在严重的数值基准记忆问题，记忆通道导致模型产生虚假的因果关联。现有的黑盒探测方法低估了记忆泄露程度，而简单的系统提示防御可以有效缓解非自适应攻击，但对概念性和历史叙事查询的效用成本几乎为零。

Abstract: Public numeric benchmarks appear in pretraining, so an evaluation that conditions on a date may be measuring memorized recall rather than out-of-sample skill. We introduce NumLeak, a measurement framework that combines API-boundary probes on production models with a white-box controlled validation on an open causal LM. Top-tier frontier LLMs recall the Fama-French market excess return at 3-seed pooled Pearson r=0.97-0.99 while staying within 0.15 within-25bps on the five sibling factors; comparable fidelity appears on U.S. unemployment, CPI inflation, and NOAA temperature. On a recent-release holdout, parse rate collapses to 21-57% but r stays at approximately 0.99 on months answered, the refuse-or-recall asymmetry a memorized channel predicts. The white-box experiment reproduces the dose-response, and logprob ranking detects memorization that open-ended generation misses, implying closed-API black-box probes understate the channel. A Sonnet "date to market-sentiment" regression that correlates with true Mkt-RF at r=0.74 collapses to r=0.02 once the model's own recall is residualized out. A one-line system-prompt defense blocks 99.8% of a non-adaptive single-turn suffix attack set at near-zero utility cost on conceptual and historical-narrative queries

</details>


### [99] [LongDS-Bench: On the Failure of Long-Horizon Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30434)
*Kewei Xu,Xiaoben Lu,Shuofei Qiao,Zihan Ding,Haoming Xu,Lei Liang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongDS是一个面向长程、多轮数据分析的基准测试，包含68个基于Kaggle笔记构建的任务（共2,225轮），要求智能体在长交互过程中维护、更新、恢复和组合演化的分析状态。评估五个先进模型，最佳准确率仅48.45%，从早期到后期回合性能下降近47个百分点，长程错误占失败的52%-69%，且增加交互步数未必提升性能，关键瓶颈在于维护正确的分析状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估孤立或短交互任务，缺乏对智能体在长程分析中追踪和维持演化上下文能力的测试，因此需要构建一个专门评估长程、多轮数据分析中状态演化能力的基准。

Method: 从真实Kaggle笔记构建68个长程多轮任务，涵盖地理科学、商业、教育等6个领域共2,225轮交互，围绕状态演化模式（如反事实扰动、回滚、多状态组合）设计任务，平均依赖跨度11.3轮。

Result: 最佳模型（GPT-4等）平均准确率仅48.45%，早期到后期回合性能下降近47个百分点，长程错误占失败的52%-69%，且额外增加交互步数未必提升性能。

Conclusion: 长程数据分析的关键瓶颈在于维护正确的分析状态而非增加交互预算，现有模型在长程分析中表现不佳，LongDS为研究可靠的长期智能体数据分析提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Real-world data analysis is inherently iterative, yet existing benchmarks mostly evaluate isolated or short interactive tasks, leaving agents' ability to track evolving analytical context over long horizons untested. We introduce LongDS, a benchmark for long-horizon, multi-turn data analysis where agents must maintain, update, restore, and compose evolving analytical states. LongDS comprises 68 tasks constructed from real-world Kaggle notebooks, spanning 2,225 turns across six domains including Geoscience, Business, and Education. Tasks are designed around state-evolution patterns (e.g., counterfactual perturbation, rollback, multi-state composition), with an average dependency span of 11.3 turns. Evaluating five state-of-the-art models, we find that the best model reaches only 48.45% average accuracy, performance drops nearly 47 points from early to late turns, and long-horizon errors account for 52%--69% of failures. Further analysis shows that additional agent steps do not necessarily improve performance, suggesting that the key bottleneck is maintaining a correct analytical state rather than increasing interaction budget. We release LongDS to support research on reliable long-horizon agentic data analysis. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/DataMind.

</details>


### [100] [Calibrated Preference Learning: The Case of Label Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30447)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Viktor Bengs,Timo Kaufmann,Sebastian J. Vollmer,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化定义了概率标签排序（Probabilistic Label Ranking）中的校准问题，建立了涵盖完整排序、子排序和Top-k排序的校准层次体系，并通过实验发现现有模型校准性差，且校准度与基准准确率相关但不完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准对于可靠决策至关重要，但此前校准研究仅针对分类和回归任务，尚未正式处理概率标签排序问题。简单地将排序视为类别会忽略其结构特性，无法捕捉成对预测和Top-k预测等重要模态。

Method: 形式化定义标签排序的校准概念，构建包含完整排序、子排序和Top-k排序的层次化校准体系，并通过理论证明三者之间的蕴含关系。在多个流行标签排序模型及RLHF奖励模型上进行实证评估。

Result: 完整排序校准蕴含子排序和Top-k校准，但反之不成立；子排序校准与Top-k校准不可比较。现有标签排序模型普遍校准性差，子排序与Top-k指标存在显著差异。RLHF奖励模型的校准度与基准准确率强相关但不完全一致。

Conclusion: 校准捕捉了超越Top-1准确率的有意义质量维度，未来工作需要理解校准不良的下游影响并开发相应的校正方法。

Abstract: Calibration, the alignment of predicted probabilities with true outcome frequencies, is essential for reliable decision-making. While extensively studied for classification and regression, calibration has not been formally addressed for probabilistic label ranking, where the goal is to predict a distribution over orderings of a label set. Naively treating rankings as classes ignores their structure and fails to capture important modalities such as pairwise and top-k predictions. We formalize calibration for label ranking and develop a hierarchy of notions covering full rankings, sub-rankings, and top-k rankings. We prove that full-rank calibration implies the others but not conversely, and sub-ranking and top-k calibration are incomparable. Empirically, we find popular label ranking models are often poorly calibrated, with substantial differences between sub-ranking and top-k metrics. Applying our framework to RLHF reward models, we find that calibration correlates strongly but not perfectly with benchmark accuracy, suggesting it captures a meaningful quality dimension beyond top-1 accuracy. These findings motivate future work on understanding the downstream effects of miscalibration and developing methods to correct it.

</details>


### [101] [Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability for Black-Box LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30448)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有界行为不可区分性（$(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-behavioral indistinguishability）来评估黑盒LLM蒸馏，发现语义相似度提升不足以保证行为不可区分，对抗性评估和类别感知分析揭示蒸馏后仍存在系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒LLM蒸馏评估仅关注输出语义相似性或任务一致性，但输出相似并不代表学生模型在行为上与教师模型不可区分，缺乏严格的行为级评估框架。

Method: 形式化定义了$(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-行为不可区分性；在Qwen和Llama师生对上进行5,000提示的行为探针套件实验；使用LoRA蒸馏，对比基础学生和蒸馏学生；采用对抗性判别器、成对类别分析、跨家族教师判别和A/B-swap一致性过滤等方法进行评估。

Result: LoRA将Qwen语义相似度从0.788提升至0.862，Llama从0.814提升至0.874；但对抗判别器仍保持非零优势，风格/格式、鲁棒性和领域技术提示中的伪影持续存在；跨家族教师判别中，Qwen的区分优势从0.158降至0.081；查询预算实验表明，分歧引导采样并不一致优于分层随机采样。

Conclusion: 语义保真度有用但不充分，黑盒LLM蒸馏需要结合有界性、对抗性和类别感知的评估方法。

Abstract: Black-box LLM distillation is usually evaluated as an output-matching problem: a student is considered successful when its responses are semantically similar to, or task-consistent with, those of a teacher. However, output similarity does not imply that the student is behaviorally indistinguishable from the model it imitates. We introduce bounded behavioral indistinguishability, formalized as $(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-behavioral indistinguishability over an explicit prompt distribution, where $ε$ bounds distinguishing advantage, $q$ bounds oracle queries, $t$ bounds computation, and $\mathbb{A}$ denotes the adversary class.
  We instantiate this notion on Qwen and Llama teacher-student pairs using a controlled $5,000$-prompt behavioral probe suite. For each family, we compare the teacher with both the base student and the LoRA-distilled student, measuring whether distillation reduces distinguishability rather than merely improving similarity. LoRA raises semantic similarity from $0.788$ to $0.862$ for Qwen and from $0.814$ to $0.874$ for Llama. Yet adversarial evaluation reveals remaining behavioral differences: learned discriminators retain nonzero advantage, and pairwise category analysis shows artifacts concentrated in style/format, robustness, and domain-technical prompts. A pairwise teacher-identification adversary confirms this trend. With a different-family Llama judge and A/B-swap consistency filtering, Qwen distinguishing advantage drops from $0.158$ for the base student to $0.081$ after LoRA distillation. Query-budget experiments show that disagreement-guided acquisition does not consistently outperform stratified random sampling, indicating that coverage and diversity remain strong baselines. Our results show that semantic fidelity is useful but insufficient: black-box LLM distillation requires bounded, adversarial, and category-aware evaluation.

</details>


### [102] [VeriGate: Verifier-Gated Step-Level Supervision for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30451)
*Aakriti Agrawal,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeriGate是一种基于验证器门控的步骤级GRPO扩展方法，通过结合结果奖励和过程监督，在结果奖励退化时引入过程奖励模型的未来累积奖励作为token级优势信号，解决了GRPO的稀疏监督和信用分配问题。在MATH数据集上使用1.5B和7B Qwen2.5-Instruct模型训练，在六个推理基准上分别提升约20%和12%的平均准确率，显著减少零梯度失败和奖励破解行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO存在两个关键局限：(1) 当所有采样轨迹获得相同的验证器奖励时，组相对优势归零，学习停滞；(2) 纯结果奖励缺乏步骤级信用分配，限制探索能力，难以学习稳健的推理过程。

Method: VeriGate包含三个核心设计：(1) 验证器门控机制：当验证器奖励能区分轨迹偏好时由验证器主导，否则切换到过程监督；(2) 未来累积奖励转换：将PRM步骤分数转化为未来累积奖励，实现延续感知的信用分配；(3) 组归一化的token级优势：将奖励转化为组归一化的token级优势信号，恢复信息梯度，同时比直接优化聚合PRM分数更不易受到奖励破解。

Result: 在MATH上使用1.5B和7B Qwen2.5-Instruct模型训练，在六个推理基准上评估：1.5B模型平均准确率提升约20%，7B模型提升约12%；显著减少零梯度失败和奖励破解行为，推理质量优于纯结果GRPO和PRM作为结果奖励的基线方法。

Conclusion: VeriGate通过验证器门控机制自适应地在结果奖励和过程监督之间切换，结合未来累积奖励和token级优势归一化，有效解决了GRPO的稀疏监督和信用分配问题，在多种规模模型上均取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is an effective recipe for training reasoning models with verifier-based outcome rewards, but its supervision is sparse: when all sampled trajectories for a prompt receive the same verifier reward, the group-relative advantage collapses to zero and learning stalls. Outcome-only rewards also provide no step-level credit assignment, limiting exploration and making it harder to learn robust reasoning. We present VeriGate (Verifier-Gated Step-Level GRPO), a verifier-gated extension of GRPO that addresses these limitations with three design choices. First, VeriGate keeps the verifier in charge whenever verifier rewards induce a meaningful preference among sampled trajectories, and uses process supervision only when verifier rewards are degenerate. Second, instead of collapsing Process Reward Model (PRM) step scores into a single trajectory reward, VeriGate converts them into future-cumulated rewards to assign continuation-aware credit. Third, VeriGate transforms these rewards into group-normalized token-level advantages, restoring informative gradients and fine-grained credit assignment while remaining less susceptible to reward hacking than methods that optimize aggregated PRM scores. Empirically, training on MATH with 1.5B and 7B Qwen2.5-Instruct models and evaluating on six reasoning benchmarks, VeriGate improves average accuracy by about 20% and 12% for 1.5B and 7B models respectively, substantially reduces zero-gradient failures, decreases reward-hacking behavior, and improves reasoning quality relative to outcome-only GRPO and PRM-as-outcome baselines.

</details>


### [103] [A Unified Framework for Gradient Aggregation in Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30452)
*Zeou Hu,Kelvin Ho,Yaoliang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了多目标优化中梯度聚合的统一框架，建立了收敛到帕累托平稳性的（最优）速率。核心是充分对齐条件，证明凸包内非冲突方向是收敛的充分条件，并通过对偶锥投影确保可行性。同时提出梯度聚合的原始优化视角，统一现有算法并设计了新变体（capped MGDA），在对抗联邦学习中验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度聚合多目标优化方法各有不同的提出动机和分析方式，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解它们的收敛性保证和相互关系。

Method: 提出充分对齐条件，证明非冲突方向（在梯度凸包内）是收敛的充分必要条件；通过对偶锥投影确保可行性；从原始优化视角统一梯度聚合方法。

Result: 建立了梯度聚合的统一收敛理论，证明了最优收敛速率；设计了capped MGDA算法，在对抗联邦学习任务中展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提供的统一框架不仅阐明了现有MOO算法的理论关系，还能指导设计新的变体，capped MGDA在对抗联邦学习中表现良好验证了理论的有效性。

Abstract: Many machine learning problems involve multiple inherent trade-offs that are best addressed by gradient-based multi-objective optimization (MOO) algorithms. Existing methods are often proposed with various motivations, analyzed case by case, and differ algorithmically in how the component gradients are aggregated at each step. In this work, we develop a unifying framework for gradient aggregation in MOO, establishing (optimal) rates of convergence to Pareto stationarity, the standard measure of performance in MOO. Central to our analysis is a sufficient alignment condition, from which we derive a theorem showing that non-conflicting directions, when chosen within the convex hull of gradients, form a fundamental sufficient condition for convergence. We further show that feasibility can be ensured through projection onto the dual cone, broadening the scope of methods that admit convergence guarantees. In parallel, we present a primal optimization perspective of gradient aggregation that encompasses established algorithms, clarifies their theoretical relationships, and enables the design of new variants. As an illustration, we introduce capped MGDA, derived from a CVaR-based formulation, and demonstrate its robustness in adversarial federated learning. Finally, we validate our theory through experiments on synthetic problems and practical benchmarks.

</details>


### [104] [Scalable Constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via State Augmentation and Consensus for Separable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30461)
*Santiago Amaya-Corredor,Miguel Calvo-Fullana,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分布式约束MARL方法，通过状态增强策略学习与对偶变量分布式共识相结合，实现全局资源约束下的多智能体协调，在满足约束的同时保持线性可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立学习无法解决全局约束下的多智能体问题，因为各智能体无法自主确定对集体约束的适当贡献份额；而现有CTDE方法复杂度随智能体数量呈二次增长，难以扩展。

Method: 每个智能体学习一个增强策略（同时以局部状态和对偶变量为输入），通过邻居间轻量级通信达成对偶变量共识，实现分布式约束满足；理论证明共识误差有界且约束违反随图连通性和共识轮次增加而减小。

Result: 在智能电网需求响应实验中，无共识时智能体会通过无限期推迟需求来满足约束（退化非解），有共识时能同时满足电网约束和需求满足；可扩展至数千智能体，而CTDE基线仅能处理数十个。

Conclusion: 邻居间对偶变量共识足以实现全局协调的约束满足，方法在训练和执行阶段均呈线性可扩展性，优于CTDE的二次复杂度。

Abstract: We present a distributed approach for constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) that combines state-augmented policy learning with distributed consensus over dual variables. Our method targets systems where agents have separable dynamics but must coordinate to satisfy global resource constraints, a setting in which, as we demonstrate empirically, independent learning fails to produce feasible solutions because agents cannot determine appropriate individual contributions toward collective constraint satisfaction. The key technical contribution is showing that lightweight neighbor-to-neighbor consensus over Lagrange multipliers suffices for globally coordinated constraint enforcement while preserving the scalability of independent training. Each agent learns a single augmented policy offline, conditioned on both its local state and a dual variable encoding constraint feedback. During execution, agents reach agreement on this dual variable through local communication alone. We prove that under mild connectivity assumptions, the consensus error among agents' multipliers is bounded, and show that this translates to a bounded constraint violation that decreases with graph connectivity and the number of consensus rounds. Unlike centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE) approaches, whose complexity grows at least quadratically with agent count, our method scales linearly in both training and execution. Experiments on smart grid demand response demonstrate that consensus coordination is \emph{essential for feasibility}: without it, agents satisfy grid capacity constraints only by indefinitely postponing demand, a degenerate non-solution. With consensus, agents converge to a shared dual variable and satisfy both grid constraints and demand fulfillment, scaling to thousands of agents while CTDE baselines are limited to dozens.

</details>


### [105] [idSCD: Identifying Training Datasets through Semantic Correlation Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30462)
*Andrada Gobeaja,Ionut Hodoroaga,Elena Burceanu,Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语义相关性描述符（SCDs）的白盒方法，通过模型内部学习的虚假相关性来识别数据集是否参与训练，在多个实验中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集成员推断方法依赖行为或分布证据（如置信度、损失、生成样本等），未充分利用模型内部学习到的语义相关性结构；本文旨在证明数据集会通过训练中内化的虚假相关性留下独特印记，并据此实现数据集级别的成员推断。

Method: 提出语义相关性描述符（SCDs）作为白盒指纹，捕捉模型学习的语义相关性结构并使其跨数据集混合可比；设计留一数据集诊断方法验证SCD可分离匹配与非匹配数据集对；基于SCD构建成员得分，仅需目标数据集的单独SCD和模型的SCD即可判断该数据集是否参与训练。

Result: 在自然语言推理、情感分类、医学文本分类三个场景中，基于SCD的分类器平均性能最高、标准差最低，优于RMIA、Attack-P、LiRA等黑盒基线和SIF白盒基线；在数据集组具有明显语义特征时，ROC-AUC相对提升超过60%。

Conclusion: 数据集成员身份可通过模型内部语义相关性结构进行追踪，该方法在语义区分明显的场景下优势尤为显著。

Abstract: Can a dataset be recognized from the spurious correlations it induces during training? We argue that datasets leave dataset-specific traces in a model's learned semantic correlation structure: incidental regularities that are predictive within a dataset, but not causal for the underlying task, can be internalized during training. We use this insight to study dataset-level membership inference, moving beyond existing methods that rely on behavioral or distributional evidence such as confidence scores, losses, margins, generated samples, or query responses. We introduce a white-box semantic fingerprinting approach based on semantic correlation descriptors (SCDs), which capture the semantic correlation structure learned by a model and make it comparable across dataset mixtures. In a controlled leave-one-dataset-out diagnostic, SCDs recover dataset-specific changes and perfectly separate matching from non-matching dataset pairs. We then propose a practical SCD-based membership score that tests whether a target dataset is part of a model's training mixture using only the model's SCD and the target dataset's standalone SCD, without requiring leave-one-dataset-out models. Across three diverse experimental settings, with dataset groups for natural language inference, emotion classification, and medical text classification, we test both the advantages and limitations of SCD-based membership inference with different degrees of semantic separation and keyword support between dataset splits. On average, the classifier based on this score achieves the highest performance and the lowest std, outperforming black-box baselines RMIA, Attack-P, and LiRA, as well as the white-box SIF baseline. These results show that dataset membership can be traced through internal semantic correlations, with the largest relative gain exceeding 60% in ROC-AUC when dataset groups expose distinct semantic particularities.

</details>


### [106] [Can Subgraph Explanations Be Weaponized to Steal Graph Neural Networks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30470)
*Ojas Nimase,Jiate Li,Yue Zhao,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对图分类的严格黑盒模型提取攻击XSTEAL，利用模型解释输出（离散标签+二值解释掩码）通过蒙特卡洛边敏感度估计和解释子图搜索实现高效模型窃取，揭示了可解释性接口的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GMLaaS平台为实现监管合规而部署可解释性接口，但这种透明度为模型提取攻击创造了可利用的脆弱性。现有攻击方法无法在严格黑盒约束（仅观察离散类标签和二值解释掩码，无概率分数、梯度或置信度值）下有效工作。

Method: 提出XSTEAL方法：(1)利用模型解释输出引导蒙特卡洛边敏感度估计，逼近决策边界，并给出Hoeffding集中不等式保证的估计精度；(2)利用解释子图高效缩小边界搜索空间。

Result: 在多个基准图数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法优于可比基线方法，验证了可解释性接口确实创造了可被利用的攻击面。

Conclusion: 可解释性接口在满足透明度要求的同时引入了安全风险，该发现可为防御机制设计和可解释AI监管政策制定提供参考。

Abstract: Graph Machine Learning as a Service (GMLaaS) platforms increasingly implement explainability interfaces to meet regulatory transparency requirements. However, this transparency creates exploitable vulnerabilities for model extraction attacks. We present the first model extraction attack specifically designed for graph classification under strict black-box constraints where the attacker observes only discrete class labels and binary explanation masks (no probability scores, gradients, or confidence values). Our method (1) uses model explanation outputs to guide Monte Carlo edge sensitivity estimation toward decision boundaries, with Hoeffding concentration guarantees on estimation accuracy and (2) exploits explanation subgraphs to efficiently narrow the boundary search space. Extensive experiments on benchmark graph datasets across multiple domains demonstrate our method's superiority over comparable baselines. These findings demonstrate that such explainability interfaces create exploitable attack surfaces, informing both defensive mechanisms and policy frameworks for explainable AI mandates. The implementation code is provided in https://github.com/LabRAI/XSTEAL/.

</details>


### [107] [Universal Multiclass Transductive Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30479)
*Steve Hanneke,Hongao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有可能无界标签空间的通用直推式在线分类问题，刻画了该设定下的可学习性，证明可学习类的最优错误率只有有界或对数增长两种可能，并引入LCLL树等新组合结构来表征可学习性，同时将结果推广到不可知论和随机过程场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在直推式在线学习（实例序列预先已知）且标签空间可能无界的设定下，概念类的可学习性及其最优错误率特征。

Method: 引入名为"Level-Constrained-Littlestone-Littlestone (LCLL) tree"的新型组合结构，结合无差异性质（indifference property）来刻画可学习性。

Result: 证明了可学习类的最优错误率只有两种可能：有界或对数增长；并将可学习性结果推广到不可知论场景以及实例序列由已知随机过程生成的场景。

Conclusion: 直推式在线分类中，可学习类的错误率增长只有有界或对数两种速率，LCLL树是其可学习性的完整刻画工具。

Abstract: We consider the problem of universal transductive online classification with a possibly unbounded label space. This setting considers online learning, with the sequence of instances (without labels) known to the learner in advance. We say a concept class $\mathcal{H}$ is learnable if there is a learning algorithm $\mathcal{A}$, such that for every realizable sequence, the number of mistakes made by $\mathcal{A}$ grows at most sublinearly with the number of predictions. We characterize the learnability of this setting and show that there are only two possible optimal rates for the learnable classes: either bounded or increasing logarithmically. We introduce a new combinatorial structure, called ``Level-Constrained-Littlestone-Littlestone (LCLL) tree'', which, along with the indifference property, characterizes the learnability. We also extend the learnability result to the agnostic case and the case where only the stochastic process that generates the instance sequence is known.

</details>


### [108] [Discovering a Zeta Map Algorithm on Dyck Paths via Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30482)
*Xiaoyu Huang,Blake Jackson,Kyu-Hwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过训练一个小型Transformer模型学习Dyck路径上的zeta映射，利用机械可解释性工具分析其学习机制，发现了一种基于层级的峰中心遍历算法（scaffolding map），并证明该算法与zeta映射等价，展示了AI辅助数学发现中可将模型行为转化为可验证的精确算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习的输出在数学发现中需要是可验证的显式构造，而非简单预测。本文旨在探索如何利用机械可解释性从训练好的小模型中提取出可理解、可证明的数学算法。

Method: 训练一个单层、单头的encoder-decoder Transformer模型学习Dyck路径上的zeta映射；使用解码器交叉注意力分析、线性探针和因果干预等机械可解释性工具分析模型内部计算机制；将解析出的信号转化为组合数学算法（scaffolding map）并给出数学证明。

Result: 发现模型采用层级（level-based）机制：编码器表示使路径层级线性可访问，解码器以结构化方式选择和遍历输入位置。提取出的scaffolding map是一种显式的峰中心遍历算法，并证明其与zeta映射在标签翻转约定下等价。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个受控的AI辅助数学发现案例，展示了机械可解释性如何将训练模型的行为转化为精确、人类可验证的组合算法，为机器学习在数学发现中的应用提供了可解释性视角。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly used in mathematical discovery, but in mathematics the desired output is often not a prediction itself, but an explicit construction that can be checked independently. We study this setting through the zeta map on Dyck paths, a classical bijection in the combinatorics of the q,t-Catalan numbers. We train a deliberately small one-layer, one-head encoder-decoder transformer on this map and analyze its learned computation using mechanistic interpretability tools, including decoder cross-attention analysis, linear probing, and causal intervention. The analysis reveals a level-based mechanism: encoder representations make path levels linearly accessible, while the decoder selects and traverses input positions in a structured way. Translating these signals into combinatorics leads to the scaffolding map, an explicit peak-centered traversal algorithm for Dyck paths. We prove that this algorithm agrees with the zeta map, modulo a reversal convention in the labeling. This gives a controlled example of AI-assisted mathematical discovery in which mechanistic interpretability turns model behavior into a precise, human-verifiable combinatorial algorithm.

</details>


### [109] [Graph-Conditioned Mixture of Graph Neural Network Experts for Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30486)
*Amirhossein Ghaffari,Saeid Sheikhi,Ekaterina Gilman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GC-MoE提出了一种基于图条件的混合专家框架，通过为每个节点分配个性化的冻结预测专家组合来提升时空预测性能，仅训练约17K参数的轻量路由模块，在四个标准基准上提升了MAE指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空预测方法通常对所有节点使用统一的骨干架构，忽略了不同图区域（如道路段）在功能类别、结构和交通行为上的异质性动态特征，因此需要节点级的专家特化机制。

Method: 提出GC-MoE框架：1) 基于图拓扑和近期交通输入窗口，为每个节点分配个性化组合的冻结预测专家；2) 结合冻结的预训练时空GNN专家与输入感知、空间上下文化的路由模块；3) 可选的有界图条件输出精炼层；4) 节点自适应ST-LoRA适配器仅作为消融诊断。

Result: 在PEMS04、PEMS07、METR-LA和PEMS-BAY四个标准基准上，GC-MoE相比零参数集成基线提升了MAE，同时实现了有竞争力的RMSE和MAPE；仅训练约17K参数，冻结了1.5M专家权重。

Conclusion: 图条件混合专家框架通过轻量级路由实现节点级专家特化，能够有效捕捉传感器图上不同区域的异质性动态，以极小训练开销提升预测精度。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal forecasting on sensor graphs is commonly tackled with a single backbone architecture applied uniformly across all nodes, although graph regions can exhibit different dynamics. Road segments differ in functional class, structure, and traffic behavior, suggesting that node-wise expert specialization can be useful. We propose GC-MoE, a graph-conditioned mixture of experts framework that assigns each node a personalized combination of frozen forecasting experts based on graph topology and the recent traffic input window. GC-MoE combines frozen pretrained spatio-temporal GNN experts with an input-aware, spatially contextualized router while training only a lightweight routing module. We also study a bounded graph-conditioned output refinement layer as an optional extension and include node-adaptive ST-LoRA adapters only as an ablation diagnostic. Across four standard benchmarks (PEMS04, PEMS07, METR-LA, and PEMS-BAY), GC-MoE improves MAE over a zero-parameter ensemble baseline, with competitive RMSE and MAPE, while training only ~17K parameters on top of 1.5M frozen expert weights. The implementation is available at https://github.com/Ahghaffari/gc_moe.

</details>


### [110] [MAAT: Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30514)
*Suryash Yagnik,Shubham Gaur,Saksham Thakur,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有机器遗忘评估存在结构性偏差：Why类因果知识占比极低（<0.06%-1.3%），导致模型在因果知识上的失败无法被检测。本文提出5WBENCH平衡基准（5类各1000样本），发现现有方法无法同时实现Why类数据的高遗忘与高保留。为此提出MAAT框架（多阶段适配器感知定向遗忘），通过梯度投影上升、SVD秩剪枝、任务向量消去、混合KL-隐藏状态保留修复，首次在Pareto前沿上实现Why类因果知识的高遗忘与高保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估数据集（CounterFact、ZSRE、TOFU、MUSE、WMDP-Cyber）中Why类型（因果和关系知识）问题占比极低（不到0.06%-1.3%），导致即使模型在因果知识上完全失败，聚合指标仍可得分很高，这种失败无法通过不平衡评估检测。

Method: 提出5WBENCH（5000样本平衡基准，每类1000样本）评估现有方法；提出MAAT框架：三阶段操作于LoRA适配器权重，包括梯度投影上升（forgetting）、SVD秩维剪枝、任务向量消去、以及混合KL散度-隐藏状态保留修复（retention）。

Result: 现有基线方法无法在Why类问题上同时实现高遗忘和高保留：激进遗忘破坏保留知识，保守方法无法遗忘因果事实。Why类困难源于多跳推理链（44% vs ≤2%）和40.1 token长答案跨度导致的梯度稀释。MAAT首次在Pareto前沿上实现Why类知识的高遗忘与高保留。

Conclusion: 机器遗忘评估需引入Why类因果知识的平衡评估；MAAT为解决因果知识遗忘-保留权衡的有效方法。

Abstract: Machine unlearning evaluation is structurally skewed: Why-type questions, which probe causal and relational knowledge, comprise less than 0.06% of CounterFact, 0.6% of ZSRE, and less than 1.3% of TOFU, MUSE, and WMDP-Cyber. This near-zero representation means that methods that fail on causal knowledge can score highly in aggregate, and this failure is undetectable without balanced evaluation. We present 5WBENCH, a balanced 5,000-sample benchmark with 1,000 examples per 5W category (Who, What, When, Where, Why), making causal unlearning failures quantifiable for the first time. Using 5WBENCH, we show that no existing baseline simultaneously achieves high forgetting and high retention on Why-type questions: aggressive forgetting degrades retained knowledge, while conservative methods fail to forget causal facts. Why-type difficulty stems from multi-hop reasoning chains (44% of Why entries vs. less than or equal to 2% for others) and gradient dilution over 40.1-token answer spans.
