Notable examples of two-timescale SA in reinforcement learning (RL) include temporal difference learning with gradient correction (TDC) and actor-critic methods. Previously, the stability (i.e., boundedness) and convergence of two-timescale SA were only established under i.i.d. noise. This work instead establishes the stability and convergence of two-timescale SA under Markovian noise, a setup that is more realistic in RL. Notably, we do not need to use any projection operator and the noise does not need to live in a compact space. Our key technical novelty is to control the fast timescale parameter with the running max of the slow timescale parameter, instead of with the current slow timescale parameter, as most prior works do. As a key application, we establish the first almost sure convergence of TDC with eligibility traces under off-policy learning with linear function approximation.

</details>


### [186] [Retriever Portfolios: A Principled Approach to Adaptive RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31176)
*Miltiadis Stouras,Vincent Cohen-Addad,Silvio Lattanzi,Ola Svensson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自动选择少量多样检索器组合（Portfolio）的方法，以覆盖异构查询分布，通过期望最优目标与高效构建算法，在QA基准上超越单检索器和朴素多检索器基线，且相比推理时调参，固定组合能实现并行检索，降低延迟与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统通常使用单一检索器和固定超参数，但实际查询高度异构（从简单事实到复杂多跳推理），单一方案难以覆盖全部查询分布。

Method: 从大量候选检索器中自动选取一个小的多样化子集（portfolio），形式化为查询分布上的期望最优目标（expected best-of-k），并设计具有近优保证的高效构建算法；同时训练路由管线对每个查询动态选择组合中的最佳检索器。

Result: 在多个QA基准上，学习得到的portfolios和路由管线在检索指标和答案质量上一致优于单检索器和朴素多检索器基线；相比推理时调参，固定portfolios可实现并行检索与LLM调用，在相当或更优的准确率下显著降低延迟与token成本。

Conclusion: 通过学习少量多样化检索器的固定组合，可以高效覆盖异构查询分布，在保证准确率的同时大幅提升推理效率，是一种兼顾效果与成本的RAG优化方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems typically rely on a single retriever and a single set of hyperparameters, despite facing highly heterogeneous queries that range from simple factoid questions to complex multi-hop reasoning. We propose a method that automatically selects a small, diverse subset of retrievers (a portfolio) from a large pool of candidates, to cover different regions of the target query distribution. We formalize this setting via an expected best-of-$k$ objective over the query distribution and show that it admits an efficient portfolio construction algorithm with near-optimal guarantees. Across multiple QA benchmarks, our learned portfolios and router pipeline consistently outperform single-retriever and naive multi-retriever baselines on both retrieval metrics and answer quality. In addition, compared to inference-time hyperparameter tuning approaches, fixed portfolios enable parallel retrieval and LLM calls, achieving comparable (and sometimes better) accuracy with substantially lower latency and token cost.

</details>


### [187] [How well does Classification Accuracy capture Concept Drift Detection Quality? An overview of Concept Drift Detection evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31186)
*Joanna Komorniczak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了合成非平稳数据流中漂移检测质量指标与分类性能之间的关系，通过七种数据流生成工具和八种漂移检测质量指标的实验，旨在建立评估概念漂移检测的统一框架，并识别最具信息量的评估指标组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学界尚未建立评估概念漂移检测任务的统一框架，现有研究常依赖分类质量指标，但这些指标受多种因素影响，无法可靠反映漂移检测的真实质量。

Method: 研究八种漂移检测质量指标与分类器性能之间的关系，使用七种合成数据流生成工具，并将漂移动态作为影响因素。

Result: 确定了最具信息量的漂移检测质量指标集合，并深入理解了评估方法。

Conclusion: 为评估概念漂移检测提供了统一的框架指导，有助于更可靠地衡量漂移检测方法的真实质量。

Abstract: Data streams are nowadays among the most frequently analyzed data structures, with the concept drift posing a major challenge encountered by processing systems. Despite the proposition of numerous solutions to counteract the accuracy degeneration due to concept drift, the scientific community has not yet established a unified framework for evaluating the concept drift detection task. Existing research often relies on classification quality metrics, but these can be affected by multiple factors and may not reliably reflect drift detection quality. In this work, we present an in-depth overview of the relationship between metrics for quantifying drift detection quality and classification performance in synthetic nonstationary data streams. The proposed research studies eight drift detection quality metrics in relation to the classifier's performance across seven synthetic data stream generation tools, additionally considering drift dynamics as a factor. The studies aim to identify the most informative set of drift detection quality metrics and provide a deep understanding of the method's evaluation.

</details>


### [188] [FlagGAM: Rule-Based Generalized Additive Modeling for Explainable Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31189)
*Zijie Zhao,Roy E. Welsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlagGAM提出了一种基于规则定义基函数的框架，通过分离特征级规则构建与预测，生成稀疏、可读的独热规则基矩阵，在保持透明性的同时提升了对缺失和噪声输入的鲁棒性，并能在灵活预测头下接近强树模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域中的表格预测任务需要模型同时具备准确性、透明性和对不完美输入的鲁棒性，现有方法难以兼顾这三者。

Method: 提出FlagGAM框架，包含Flag Core Module（将数值和类别变量转换为稀疏、可读的单变量基函数，包括阈值标志、类别级标志、尾部偏差基和类别阶梯函数）和默认加性预测头（以受限GAM方式组合基函数），保留稀疏规则基矩阵而非压缩为计数汇总，支持混合类型分类回归、特征特定权重和灵活预测头。

Result: 默认FlagGAM在透明加性模式下接近EBM性能，在混合类型回归上显著优于岭回归；在缺失和噪声扰动下AUROC下降幅度小于常见基线；灵活预测头进一步提升精度，接近强树模型基线。

Conclusion: FlagGAM为需要竞争性精度、可沟通规则和对不完美输入鲁棒性的表格预测任务提供了一个实用的折中方案。

Abstract: Tabular prediction in high-stakes domains requires models that are accurate, transparent, and robust to imperfect inputs. We propose FlagGAM, a rule-defined basis framework that separates feature-level rule construction from prediction. A Flag Core Module converts numerical and categorical variables into sparse, human-readable univariate bases, including threshold flags, category-level flags, tail-deviation bases, and categorical step functions; a default additive head then combines these bases as a restricted GAM-style predictor. Rather than reducing triggered rules to compact count summaries, FlagGAM retains a sparse rule-basis matrix that supports mixed-type classification and regression, feature-specific weighting, and optional flexible prediction heads. Across tabular benchmarks, default FlagGAM remains close to EBM in transparent additive mode, improves substantially over ridge regression on mixed-type regression, and shows smaller AUROC degradation than common baselines under missing and noisy perturbations. Flexible heads further improve accuracy and approach strong tree-based baselines, with the caveat that the resulting model should be interpreted as a rule-basis representation followed by a nonlinear predictor rather than as a fully additive GAM. Overall, FlagGAM provides a practical middle ground for tabular settings that require competitive accuracy, communicable rules, and robustness to imperfect inputs.

</details>


### [189] [Student Capacity Moderates Knowledge Distillation Effectiveness: A Systematic Study Across ResNet Teacher-Student Pairs on CIFAR-10](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31191)
*Umut Onur Yasar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了知识蒸馏中师生模型容量关系对蒸馏效果的影响，在CIFAR-10上使用ResNet架构进行了可控实验。主要发现：学生容量是蒸馏增益的关键调节因素；梯度裁剪bug严重影响了特征蒸馏的性能对比；输入分辨率感知架构是有效蒸馏的前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究师生模型容量关系如何调节知识蒸馏的有效性，澄清此前研究中存在的混淆因素（如实现错误、架构不匹配等），在受控可复现条件下对Logit-KD和Feature-KD进行公平比较。

Method: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，使用ResNet架构构建三组师生对（R50->R18, R34->R18, R50->R34），对比Logit-KD和Feature-KD两种蒸馏方法。采用3个随机种子进行实验，报告均值±标准差，所有代码开源。

Result: 1) R34学生从KD中获益显著大于R18学生（R50->R34 Feature-KD增益+0.30pp vs R34->R18 Feature-KD增益+0.18pp）；2) 修正梯度裁剪bug后，Feature-KD在两组师生对中匹配或超越Logit-KD（R50->R34达95.55% vs 基线95.25%）；3) 修正输入分辨率架构使教师准确率提升超过5pp，远超任何KD增益。

Conclusion: 学生容量是蒸馏效果的显著调节因素；实现正确性（如梯度裁剪）对特征蒸馏至关重要；输入分辨率适配架构是有效蒸馏的必要前提。

Abstract: We investigate how teacher-student capacity relationships modulate knowledge distillation (KD) effectiveness in ResNet-based image classification on CIFAR-10. Across three teacher-student pairs -- R50->R18, R34->R18, and R50->R34 -- we compare Logit-KD and Feature-KD under controlled, reproducible conditions (3 seeds, mean+/-std reported throughout). We report three main findings. First, student capacity is a key moderating factor in distillation gain: R34 students benefit substantially more from KD than R18 students even when teacher-student accuracy gaps are comparable, with the strongest gain of +0.30pp observed for R50->R34 Feature-KD versus +0.18pp for R34->R18 Feature-KD and +0.00pp for R34->R18 Logit-KD. Second, implementation correctness critically affects Feature-KD: a gradient clipping bug that excluded projection layers suppressed Feature-KD performance and produced misleading comparisons with Logit-KD. After correction, Feature-KD matches or outperforms Logit-KD in two of three pairs, reaching 95.55% on R50->R34 against a baseline of 95.25%. Third, input-resolution-aware architecture is a prerequisite for effective distillation: correcting the ResNet stem for 32x32 inputs raises teacher accuracy by over 5pp -- an order of magnitude larger than any KD gain. All code and results are available at github.com/umutonuryasar/kd-capacity-gap.

</details>


### [190] [Geometry-based Schrödinger Bridges for Trustworthy Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31193)
*Jiayu Xiong,Jing Wang,Qi Zhang,Wanlong Wang,Jun Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于几何的多模态融合方法（GMF），利用扩散薛定谔桥传输的平方初始速度作为独立于模型预测的可靠性度量，有效应对传感器噪声、不完整数据和语义冲突等低质量多模态数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信融合方法依赖模型自身置信度来判断数据质量，形成循环依赖：当模型自信但错误时，这些方法无法检测到错误。需要打破这一循环，建立独立于预测的可靠性评估机制。

Method: 提出GMF方法，通过测量输入在潜空间中所需的传输校正量来评估可靠性。采用带整流流的扩散薛定谔桥传输，将平方初始速度作为高效学习的校正分数；有效数据具有低平方速度量级，而噪声、不完整或冲突数据需要更强的传输校正。

Result: 实验表明，GMF在严重传感器噪声和语义冲突场景下，相比基于置信度的基线方法显著提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于几何的可靠性信号可作为独立于分类器预测的公正评判者，有效标记不可靠输入，打破循环依赖问题，提升多模态融合的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal systems must be robust against low-quality data, such as sensor noise, incomplete multimodal data and conflicting inputs. However, existing trustworthy fusion methods rely on the model's own prediction confidence to judge data quality. This creates a circular dependency: when a model is confident but wrong, these methods fail to detect the error. To break this loop, we propose Geometry-based Multimodal Fusion (GMF). Instead of relying on predictions, we evaluate reliability by measuring how much transport correction the input needs in latent space. We implement Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge transport with Rectified Flow, where the squared initial velocity gives an efficient learned correction score. Valid data has low squared velocity magnitude, while noisy, incomplete data or conflicting data requires stronger transport correction. This geometry-based reliability signal acts as an independent judge, effectively flagging unreliable inputs even when the classifier is fooled. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GMF significantly improves robustness against severe sensor noise and semantic conflicts compared to confidence-based baselines.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Additive Decompositions: Interpretability Through Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31200)
*Jinyang Liu,Munir Eberhardt Hiabu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出张量分离学习（TSL）回归模型，通过秩1乘积和贪婪正交重拟合避免加性模型中的信号抵消和外推问题，实现可解释且结构忠实于数据的机器学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释方法（如GAMs、SHAP、功能ANOVA）依赖加性表示，在存在强交互时会出现信号抵消和超出支持范围的外推问题，导致信息损失。

Method: TSL模型将回归函数学习为单变量特征函数的秩1乘积之和，采用阶段贪婪过程结合正交重拟合；通过强制执行可分离性保留高阶交互信息。

Result: 建立了具有有界混合p阶偏导数的函数的逼近率保证；在回归基准测试中TSL与黑盒模型性能相当。

Conclusion: TSL避免了加性投影中由于边缘化高阶交互导致的信息损失，学习到的模型可仅由一阶偏依赖函数完全重构（至常数因子），确保了可视化对拟合组件的忠实性。

Abstract: Interpretable machine learning requires models that are accurate and structurally faithful to the data.Existing explainability methods rely heavily on additive representations (e.g., Generalized Additive Models (GAMs), SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), functional ANOVA), which can suffer from signal cancellation and off-support extrapolation in the presence of strong interactions. We propose Tensor Separation Learning (TSL), a regression model that learns a sum of rank-1 products of univariate per-feature functions via a stagewise greedy procedure with orthogonal refitting. By enforcing separability, TSL avoids the information loss inherent in additive projections caused by marginalizing higher-order interactions. The learned TSL model can be fully reconstructed from first-order partial dependence functions, up to constant factors. This stage-wise correspondence ensures that the resulting visualizations are faithful to the fitted components. We establish approximation-rate guarantees for functions with bounded mixed $p$-th order partial derivatives and demonstrate that TSL competes with black-box models on regression benchmarks.

</details>


### [192] [Fixed-Point Masked Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31215)
*Andrea Miele,Yiming Qin,Alba Carballo-Castro,Justin Deschenaux,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoFRe框架，通过将固定点求解器融入掩码生成模型的去噪器，并引入跨步一致性损失和三态重用的热身策略，实现了自适应深度、更少参数和更低计算成本，在文本和图像生成上均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码生成模型（MGMs）在每个去噪步骤都需要完整序列的双向Transformer，导致训练成本高、低采样预算下质量差。虽然已有工作通过更好的采样器或固定深度去噪器提升效率，但每步仍分配固定计算量，缺乏自适应调节能力。

Method: 提出固定点掩码生成模型（FP-MGMs），用固定点求解器替代部分去噪器，在共享注意力层上实现自适应深度和更少参数。关键组件包括：(1) 跨步一致性损失，对齐相邻去噪步骤的隐藏表示；(2) 三态重用（3SR），针对未变化、仍掩码和新揭示三种token分别初始化求解器状态；(3) 支持从预训练MGMs微调转换，避免从头训练。

Result: 在OpenWebText上，参数量减少38.8%，训练时间减少11.5%，显存减少16.9%，生成困惑度从830.8降至101.8（96步预算下）。在ImageNette上，训练时间减少48.6%，显存减少50.7%，所有采样预算下FID均有改善。

Conclusion: CoFRe提供了一种实用的训练到推理框架，通过固定点求解器实现自适应深度，在降低训练成本的同时提升了低预算下的生成质量，并兼容预训练模型的高效微调迁移。

Abstract: Masked Generative Models (MGMs) enable parallel decoding and achieve strong performance across modalities, but require full-sequence bidirectional transformers at every step, making training costly and degrading quality under low sampling budgets. Existing work improves efficiency via better samplers or cheaper fixed-depth denoisers, but they still allocate a fixed amount of denoiser computation to each refinement step. We introduce Fixed-Point Masked Generative Models (FP-MGMs), which replace part of the denoiser with a fixed-point solver over shared attention layers to enable adaptive depth with fewer parameters. To make it more effective for masked generation, we first introduce a cross-step consistency loss, which aligns hidden representations at neighboring denoising steps and, second, three-state reuse (3SR) which warm-starts the solver using the previous solution by treating differently unchanged, still-masked, and newly revealed tokens respectively. Together, these components define our complete training-to-inference framework for fixed-point masked generation, \emph{CoFRe}. We also show that pre-trained MGMs can be converted into FP-MGMs with short fine-tuning, avoiding full retraining. Across modalities, CoFRe improves the quality and cost trade-off. On OpenWebText, CoFRe reduces parameters by 38.8\%, training time by 11.5\%, and VRAM by 16.9\%, while improving generative perplexity from 830.8 to 101.8 at a budget of $96$ transformer-block forward passes, compared to MDLM. In ImageNette, CoFRe reduces training time by 48.6\% and VRAM by 50.7\%, while improving FID in all sample budgets tested. Overall, CoFRe offers a practical framework for cheaper training and stronger low-budget masked generation.

</details>


### [193] [Trading Complexity for Expressivity Through Structured Generalized Linear Token Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31367)
*Erwan Fagnou,Paul Caillon,Blaise Delattre,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将token混合层解耦为直接影响和循环传播两个关键特征，从而设计新的循环模式，在运行时间与表达能力之间实现有原则的权衡，并通过合成任务和语言建模进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token混合层（如注意力机制和状态空间模型）在解码速度、内存需求和缓存大小之间存在必要的权衡，缺乏一个统一的视角来理解和设计这些层。

Method: 提出一个统一框架，将token混合层解耦为两个关键特征：(i) 输入对输出的直接影响；(ii) 通过过去输出的循环信息传播。该框架不仅涵盖注意力和状态空间模型，还通过允许每个状态依赖多个过去状态来泛化循环方程，并设计了新的循环模式以在理论上保证所需的复杂度。

Result: 新设计的循环模式在理论上实现了期望的复杂度，并在合成任务和语言建模上进行了实证验证，展示了运行时间与表达能力之间的有原则权衡。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的工具包，用于跨模型家族理解和设计高效且富有表达力的token混合器。

Abstract: Token mixing layers play a key role in how language models can learn and generate long-range dependencies. Their efficiency relies on the necessary trade-off between decoding speed and the memory requirements, along with the cache size. Considering causal generation, this paper explores new trade-offs thanks to a unified framework which separates two crucial features: (i) the direct influence of inputs on outputs in one generation step; (ii) the recurrent propagation of information through past outputs. This framework encompasses major architectures such as attention and state-space models, but also generalizes the recurrence equations by allowing each state to depend on multiple past states rather than only the immediate predecessor. By introducing structure, we design new recurrence patterns that provably achieve the desired complexity, while providing theoretical insights on their expressivity -- trading runtime for expressivity in a principled way. Empirical validation is performed on synthetic tasks, along with language modeling. Together, these results provide a unified toolkit for the understanding and design of efficient and expressive token mixers across model families.

</details>


### [194] [Multivariate Distributional Reinforcement Learning Using Sliced Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31222)
*Baptiste Debes,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出切片分布强化学习（SDRL），通过将一维散度投影到多维上实现多变量返回分布建模，证明了均匀切片和最大切片变体下的贝尔曼收缩性，并分析了Wasserstein、Cramér和MMD等基散度的适用性，在玩具链问题、网格世界和部分Atari游戏上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布强化学习（DRL）主要处理一维返回分布，扩展到多变量场景面临诸多挑战：许多常见度量无法自然推广到多维，或在多维情况下计算不可行；多变量情况下引入的通用矩阵折扣尚无收缩性结果。因此亟需一种能有效处理多变量返回分布的方法。

Method: 提出SDRL框架，通过将一维散度沿随机投影方向投影到多维返回分布上，实现多变量分布度量。设计了均匀切片（uniform slicing）和最大切片（maximum-slicing）两种变体，分别针对标量折扣和通用密集折扣矩阵证明了贝尔曼收缩性。分析了Wasserstein距离、Cramér距离和最大均值差异（MMD）三种基散度，并刻画了哪些SDRL变体适用于分布强化学习中常用的单样本贝尔曼更新。

Result: 在玩具链问题、基于图像的网格世界环境以及部分Atari游戏上的实验评估了SDRL的性能。

Conclusion: SDRL通过切片投影方式有效将一维分布散度扩展到多变量场景，解决了多变量分布强化学习中的关键理论问题（贝尔曼收缩性），并支持多种基散度和折扣设置，为多变量分布强化学习提供了通用且理论完备的框架。

Abstract: Distributional reinforcement learning (DRL) models the full return distribution rather than expectations, but extending it to multivariate settings remains challenging. Many common metrics do not naturally generalize beyond one dimension or lose computational tractability, and the multivariate case introduces additional difficulties such as general matrix discounting, for which no contraction results are available. We introduce Sliced Distributional Reinforcement Learning (SDRL), which lifts tractable one-dimensional divergences to multivariate return distributions via projections. We prove Bellman contraction for uniform slicing under shared scalar discounting, and introduce a maximum-slicing variant with contraction under general dense discount matrices. SDRL supports a broad class of base divergences; we analyze Wasserstein, Cramér, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), and characterize which SDRL variants suit the standard single-sample Bellman update used in distributional RL. We evaluate SDRL on a toy chain problem and a gridworld image-based environment as well as a subset of Atari games.

</details>


### [195] [DRIFT: Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning for Efficient Multi-Turn Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31455)
*Jian Mu,Tianyi Lin,Chengwei Qin,Zhongxiang Dai,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DRIFT框架，通过解耦轨迹采样与优化，将KL正则化RL目标等价转化为重要性加权监督学习，在保持SFT训练效率的同时达到了在线强化学习的多轮交互性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习虽能有效处理多轮动态交互，但每次更新需生成完整校正轨迹，成本过高；而离线监督微调虽高效，却面临分布偏移和行为崩溃问题。现有方法无法兼顾效率与性能。

Method: DRIFT将KL正则化RL目标等价转化为重要性加权监督学习，从固定参考策略离线采样交互轨迹，计算基于回报的重要性权重，然后通过加权SFT优化策略，实现解耦的轨迹采样与策略优化。

Result: DRIFT在多轮交互任务上达到或超越了多轮强化学习基准的性能，同时保持了标准监督微调的训练效率和简洁性。

Conclusion: DRIFT通过理论驱动的框架设计，有效解决了多轮交互场景中在线RL昂贵与离线SFT不稳定的两难问题，为大语言模型的多轮交互优化提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in multi-turn interactive settings where users or environments can iteratively provide lightweight feedback. Unfortunately, optimizing such behavior presents a sharp dilemma in practice: online reinforcement learning is able to effectively address multi-turn dynamics but is prohibitively expensive due to the cost of generating full correction trajectories at every update, whereas offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is efficient but suffers from distribution shift and behavioral collapse. To this end, we novelly propose DRIFT (Decoupled Rollouts and Importance-Weighted Fine-Tuning), a framework that operationalizes the theoretical insight that the KL-regularized RL objective is equivalent to importance-weighted supervised learning. DRIFT decouples rollout from optimization by sampling offline interaction trajectories from a fixed reference policy, deriving return-based importance weights, and optimizing the policy via weighted SFT on the resulting dataset. Empirically, we demonstrate that DRIFT matches or exceeds the performance of multi-turn reinforcement learning baselines while maintaining the training efficiency and simplicity of standard supervised fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/DRIFT.

</details>


### [196] [Spectral Reach: Understanding Neural Scaling as Progress into the Spectral Tail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31244)
*Konstantin Nikolaou,Jonas Scheunemann,Sven Krippendorf,Samuel Tovey,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"谱位置"这一可扩展度量，用于分析神经正切核的特征值在训练中如何驱动损失下降。发现训练中学习从主导特征模式转向谱尾部，且更大模型具有更深的"谱到达"能力，能持续学习弱谱信号，而特征学习通过自适应放大梯度是这一能力的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律已广泛指导基础模型开发，但其背后的机制仍不明确，主要缺乏可扩展的分析工具。

Method: 提出"谱位置"（spectral position）度量，基于经验神经正切核（eNTK）的特征值，衡量哪些特征模式当前驱动损失下降。

Result: 训练中谱位置下降，学习从主导特征模式转向谱尾部；更大模型的"谱到达"更深，能持续学习弱谱信号；特征学习通过自适应放大梯度维持这一过程。

Conclusion: 揭示了更大模型为何能实现更低损失——它们能持续学习弱谱信号，而特征学习是实现这一能力的关键，为架构和优化器设计提供了具体干预方向。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe predictable power-law relationships between model size, dataset size, compute, and performance. While these laws guide the development of modern foundation models, the mechanisms underpinning them remain poorly understood, in part due to the absence of scalable analysis tools. To close this gap, we introduce "spectral position": a scalable measure of which eigenvalues of the empirical neural tangent kernel (eNTK) currently drive loss reduction. Applying this measure to scaling experiments, we find that spectral position decreases throughout training: learning shifts from dominant eigenmodes into the spectral tail. Larger models reach further into the tail than smaller models, revealing a size-dependent capacity we call "spectral reach". This suggests why larger models achieve lower losses: they sustain learning on weak spectral signals inaccessible to smaller models. We further identify feature learning as a key enabler of spectral reach. It adaptively amplifies gradient magnitudes as learning advances, sustaining progress where frozen representations stall. This points to concrete interventions through architecture and optimizer design.

</details>


### [197] [Lightweight CNN-Based Anomaly Detection for High Voltage Converter Modulators in the Spallation Neutron Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31259)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Julián Luengo,Isaac Triguero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高功率脉冲变换器非计划停机问题，通过改变深度学习架构中时序滤波与跨通道混合的顺序，并引入自适应通道重加权机制，在SNS的HVCM数据集上实现了AUC-PR 0.816和AUC-ROC 0.934，超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率脉冲变换器的非计划停机是大型加速器设施停机的主要来源，SNS的HVCM是导致束流时间损失的第二大因素。现有深度学习方法从第一层就将时序和跨通道操作纠缠在一起，缺乏显式建模通道独立性和结构化交互的机制，因此需要探索架构归纳偏置对检测性能的影响。

Method: 改变时序滤波和跨通道混合两种操作的执行顺序，并引入每脉冲自适应通道重加权机制。通过对比不同操作顺序的变体，评估通道重加权对故障检测灵敏度的提升效果。

Result: 在SNS四个子系统（RFQ、DTL、CCL、SCL）的公开HVCM数据集上，最佳变体实现池化AUC-PR 0.816和AUC-ROC 0.934，在大部分子系统和六个故障家族中的五个上超越现有最优方法。消融实验识别出三个主导输入通道，并揭示了各故障家族的检测性能与其前兆表现为单通道幅值偏移还是需要联合通道表征的细微模式之间的关联。

Conclusion: 架构中的归纳偏置（时序滤波与跨通道混合的顺序）对多通道脉冲信号故障检测性能具有关键影响，自适应通道重加权可进一步提升检测灵敏度。不同故障类型的检测难度与其前兆信号的表达形式密切相关。

Abstract: Unscheduled trips of high-power pulsed converters are a leading source of downtime at large accelerator facilities. At the Spallation Neutron Source (SNS), the High Voltage Converter Modulators (HVCMs) are consistently the second-largest contributor to lost beam time. Each HVCM pulse is recorded across sensor channels spanning currents, voltages, and magnetic fluxes, whose mutual interactions encode the operating state of the system. Fault precursors do not manifest uniformly across these channels: depending on fault type, they may alter the temporal structure of individual signals, change the statistical dependencies among channels, or both. Existing deep-learning approaches typically process multi-channel signals with standard convolutional pipelines that entangle temporal and cross-channel operations from the first layer, giving the model no explicit mechanism to represent channel independence or structured inter-channel interaction. We hypothesise that architectural inductive bias, specifically the ordering of temporal filtering and cross-channel mixing, plays a central role in detection performance on this class of data. To test this, we vary the order in which these two operations are applied, and examine whether per-pulse adaptive channel reweighting further improves sensitivity. Evaluated on the public HVCM dataset across all four SNS subsystems (RFQ, DTL, CCL, SCL), our best variant achieves a pooled AUC-PR of 0.816 and AUC-ROC of 0.934, outperforming the state of the art on most subsystems and five of the six fault families. Ablations identify three dominant input channels and link per-fault-family performance to whether precursors manifest as amplitude shifts in individual channels or as subtler patterns requiring joint channel representations to surface.

</details>


### [198] [Algorithmic Recourse of In-Context Learning for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31272)
*Wenshuo Dong,Jiaming Zhang,Shaopneg Fu,Hongbin Lin,Di Wang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了上下文学习（ICL）下表格数据的算法反事实解释（recourse），提出了ASR-ICL框架，通过零阶优化高效生成稀疏且可操作的反事实，并在理论和实验上证明了其收敛性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险决策场景（如信用审批）中，表格数据预测模型广泛应用，而受影响的个体需要事后可解释性（如反事实解释）。尽管ICL已用于表格预测，但针对ICL的反事实解释尚未被探索。

Method: 提出ASR-ICL（Adaptive Subspace Recourse for In-Context Learning），一种零阶优化框架，在低维自适应子空间中搜索反事实，支持稀疏性和可操作性，并扩展到多分类任务。

Result: 在多个真实数据集和模型上，ASR-ICL用更少的查询达到了与现有方法相当的反事实质量，并实验验证了理论预测的收敛行为。

Conclusion: ICL下的反事实解释是良定义且有界的，随上下文增大收敛到经典解；ASR-ICL高效实用，为ICL表格模型提供了可行的反事实解释方案。

Abstract: As predictive models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings such as credit approval, there is a growing need for post-hoc methods that provide recourse to affected individuals. Many such models operate on tabular data, where features correspond to real-world attributes. Recently, in-context learning (ICL) has enabled large language models to perform tabular prediction by conditioning on labeled examples at inference time, without explicit training. However, algorithmic recourse for tabular decision-making under ICL remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present the first study of algorithmic recourse for tabular data under ICL. We carry out a theoretical analysis, showing that recourse remains well-defined and bounded, and we characterize how recourse converges toward classical solutions as the context size increases. In practice, we propose a novel zeroth-order recourse framework, Adaptive Subspace Recourse for In-Context Learning (ASR-ICL), that efficiently generates actionable and sparse recourse for black-box ICL models. The proposed framework naturally extends to multi-class tabular tasks. Experiments across multiple real-world datasets and models demonstrate that ASR-ICL achieves recourse quality comparable to existing methods with fewer queries and empirically confirm the predicted convergence behavior, supporting our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [199] [Survival Reinforcement Learning: Toward Scalable Self-Supervised RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31273)
*Franki Nguimatsia-Tiofack,Fabian Schramm,Théotime Le Hellard,Justin Carpentier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生存强化学习（SRL），一种基于在线分类的替代方法，通过最大化智能体在目标状态的停留时间来解决对比强化学习（CRL）在长时域目标条件规划中面临的均匀性-容忍性困境。SRL在多种机器人基准测试中，操作任务上匹配SOTA CRL，在长时域 locomotion 任务上性能提升2-8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自监督对比强化学习（CRL）在长时域目标条件规划中因对比损失固有的均匀性-容忍性困境（uniformity-tolerance dilemma）而导致的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出生存强化学习（SRL），一种在线分类框架，通过最大化智能体在目标状态的停留时间（dwell time）来扩展生存价值学习框架，绕过CRL的结构约束，同时缓解生存框架中固有的"bang-bang"控制问题。

Result: 在多种机器人基准上，SRL在操作任务中匹配CRL的SOTA性能，在稳定长时域 locomotion 任务上实现2倍到8倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: 分类方法可能成为扩展强化学习的关键原语，SRL为深度RL的深度扩展提供了新的有效路径。

Abstract: While self-supervised Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) has shown remarkable depth-scaling capabilities, successfully using networks over 64 layers, scaled CRL still struggles with long-horizon goal-conditioned planning due to the uniformity-tolerance dilemma inherent in contrastive losses. We introduce Survival Reinforcement Learning (SRL), an online classification-based alternative that extends the survival value learning framework by maximizing the agent's dwell time at target goals. SRL bypasses the structural constraints of CRL and mitigates the "bang-bang" control solutions inherent to survival frameworks, which often induce undesirable behavior in complex dynamical systems. Evaluated across diverse robotic benchmarks, scaled SRL matches state-of-the-art CRL on manipulation tasks and outperforms it by 2x to 8x on stable, long-horizon locomotion tasks. Our results provide strong additional evidence that classification-based methods may serve as a key primitive in the broader effort to scale reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [200] [Learning Parametric Nitrogen Fertilizer Response Curves Using Neuro Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31276)
*Giorgio Morales,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号回归（Neuro SR）方法，无需预定义函数形式即可学习氮响应曲线。该方法结合基于Transformer的多集符号骨架预测策略，在不同管理分区中发现共享功能结构，并通过遗传算法拟合观测数据。在合成数据和真实冬小麦数据上的实验表明，该方法在数据稀缺时仍能恢复正确表达式，拟合误差低于传统二次高原和指数模型，并能捕捉不同空间区域的功能行为差异，为精准农业中的位点特异性农艺关系发现提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准农业中准确建模作物对氮肥的响应既影响经济回报又关乎环境可持续性，但现有方法要么依赖预定义参数形式，要么使用不透明的机器学习模型，限制了从数据中解释或发现位点特异性功能关系的能力。

Method: 提出神经符号回归方法，集成基于Transformer的多集符号骨架预测策略，通过构建多样化的输入子集并在子集间强制执行一致性来发现共享功能骨架，最后使用遗传算法将符号骨架拟合到观测数据上。

Result: 在合成一维问题上，该方法在数据稀缺情况下仍能恢复正确表达式；在真实冬小麦数据上，学习到的表达式不仅拟合误差低于传统二次高原和指数模型，还能捕捉不同管理分区的多样化功能行为。

Conclusion: 神经符号回归方法能够发现位点特异性的农艺关系，支撑精准农业中的明智决策，具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurately modeling crop response to Nitrogen (N) fertilization is a fundamental challenge in precision agriculture, as it impacts both economic returns and environmental sustainability. Existing approaches either rely on predefined parametric forms or opaque machine learning models, limiting their ability to interpret or discover site-specific functional relationships from data. In this work, we propose a neuro symbolic regression (SR) approach to learn parametric N-response curves without assuming a predefined functional form. Our approach integrates a transformer-based Multi-Set Symbolic Skeleton Prediction strategy, enabling the discovery of shared functional structures across multiple subdomains or management zones (MZs). By constructing diverse input subsets and enforcing consistency across them, the method recovers robust symbolic skeletons that are subsequently fitted to observed data using a genetic algorithm. This framework was first evaluated on synthetic one-dimensional problems to assess its robustness under varying levels of epistemic uncertainty. The results demonstrate the ability of the proposed SR approach to recover correct expressions even in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we present the results of applying our method to real-world winter wheat data, learning distinct parametric N-response curves for different MZs within a field. The results show that the discovered expressions not only achieve lower fitting errors than traditional models such as quadratic-plateau and exponential functions, but also capture diverse functional behaviors across spatial regions. This demonstrates the potential that neuro SR has to enable the discovery of site-specific agronomic relationships and support informed decision-making in precision agriculture.

</details>


### [201] [Interpretability Without Tradeoffs: Disentangling Polysemanticity At Equal Predictive Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31304)
*Doğukan Bağcı,Bernt Schiele,Simone Schaub-Meyer,Jonas Fischer,Robin Hesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELUDe是一种无需训练、无损的无监督解缠方法，通过重组层间信息流将DNN的潜在表示分解为可解释的单义子单元，在保证模型功能完全等价的前提下提升可解释性，且不降低下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN的单个神经元往往编码多个不相关概念，导致模型决策过程难以解释。现有方法（如稀疏自编码器）虽能分离混合信号，但通常需要修改模型结构，从而损害下游性能。

Method: ELUDe通过重组层间信息流的方式，将概念特定的贡献重新路由，在构造上保持原始计算不变，将潜在表示分解为清晰、可检查的子单元，无需显式训练、无需标签、可直接应用于预训练模型。

Result: 在多个视觉模型（包括DINOv2和有监督ViT-B/16）上，ELUDe提升了可解释性，下游准确性保持不变，运行高效，并支持模型表示引导等实际应用。

Conclusion: ELUDe提供了一种几乎无权衡的可解释性方案：在性能零损失的前提下，实现更清晰、可扩展且可操作的模型洞察。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) are widely used, but interpreting what they actually learn remains difficult. A major obstacle is that individual neurons often encode multiple unrelated concepts, obscuring the decision process of the network. While prior work, such as sparse autoencoders, can separate these mixed signals into more meaningful, "monosemantic" features, this typically requires altering the model in ways that can degrade downstream performance. To overcome this, we introduce ELUDe (explicit, lossless, unsupervised disentanglement), a method for improving the interpretability of DNNs while preserving their functional equivalence. ELUDe breaks latent representations into clear, inspectable sub-units that behave like interpretable features, while guaranteeing that the model's outputs remain exactly the same. It requires no explicit training, no labels, and can be applied to pretrained models. ELUDe works by reorganizing how information flows between layers, re-routing concept-specific contributions while preserving the original computation by construction. Across several vision models, including DINOv2 and supervised ViT-B/16, ELUDe improves interpretability, keeps downstream accuracy unchanged, runs efficiently, and supports practical uses such as steering model representations. In short, ELUDe offers interpretability (almost) without a tradeoff: clearer, scalable, and actionable model insights with no loss in performance.

</details>


### [202] [Non-Asymptotic Convergence of Stochastic Iterative Algorithms: A Lyapunov Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31309)
*Zaiwei Chen,Siva Theja Maguluri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于Lyapunov的随机迭代算法（随机逼近）的有限时间分析方法，利用广义Moreau包络作为通用Lyapunov函数，给出了在压缩算子、i.i.d.噪声下的均方收敛保证，并扩展至马尔可夫噪声、半范数压缩算子、耗散算子及高概率界，统一了随机逼近及其在强化学习中应用的有限时间分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为随机逼近算法的有限时间分析提供一个统一、自洽的理论框架，尤其是解决不同范数下Lyapunov函数构造的通用性问题，并桥接强化学习算法（如Q学习、时序差分学习）的理论分析。

Method: 利用广义Moreau包络构造通用Lyapunov函数，分析压缩算子下的随机逼近算法；从i.i.d.噪声标准情形出发，逐步扩展至马尔可夫噪声、半范数压缩算子、耗散算子，并推导高概率界。

Result: 证明了广义Moreau包络可作为任意范数下压缩算子的通用Lyapunov函数；建立了随机梯度下降、线性SA、Q学习、时序差分学习等算法的均方收敛保证；给出了马尔可夫噪声和高概率界下的扩展结果。

Conclusion: 该综述为随机逼近算法的有限时间分析提供了统一框架，展示了其在强化学习中的广泛应用，并指出了若干开放问题。

Abstract: We survey Lyapunov-based techniques for the finite-time analysis of stochastic iterative algorithms, also known as stochastic approximation (SA) algorithms, for solving fixed-point equations $\bar{F}(x)=x$, where the operator $\bar{F}(\cdot)$ can only be accessed through a noisy oracle. We first focus on the standard setting in which $\bar{F}(\cdot)$ is contractive with respect to some norm and the noise is i.i.d., and explain how generalized Moreau envelopes serve as universal Lyapunov functions, regardless of the underlying norm. We then show how this framework yields mean-square convergence guarantees and applies to stochastic gradient descent, linear SA, and value-based reinforcement learning algorithms such as Q-learning and temporal-difference learning. Finally, we discuss extensions to Markovian noise, seminorm-contractive operators, dissipative operators, and high-probability bounds, and conclude with open problems. The goal is to present a unified and self-contained roadmap for the finite-time analysis of SA and its applications, especially in reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [203] [Forgetting Has Neighbors: Localized Collateral Forgetting in Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31317)
*Polina Dolgova,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现机器遗忘存在“局部连带遗忘”现象：遗忘模型与重训练模型的预测差异在靠近遗忘集的样本上显著更大。该现象源于遗忘使用的替代目标与重训练局部预测结构不一致，并通过共享表征传播。作者提出局部教师蒸馏（Local Teacher Distillation）缓解该问题，即在遗忘集近邻上训练小教师模型生成软标签替代随机目标，显著缩小了与重训练模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评估依赖聚合指标（如准确率、遗忘分数），但这类指标会掩盖局部层面的失败模式。作者旨在揭示并量化遗忘模型在示例级别上与重训练模型的不一致，并分析其成因与缓解方法。

Method: 1. 在示例级别比较遗忘模型与重训练模型的预测差异，发现该差异在几何上靠近遗忘集的样本上显著更大（局部连带遗忘）。2. 分析机制：遗忘使用的替代目标（如梯度上升、随机标签）与重训练在该区域的局部预测结构不一致，通过共享表征传播。3. 提出局部教师蒸馏：仅在遗忘集的保留近邻上训练一个小型教师模型，将其软标签作为遗忘的替代目标。

Result: 在CIFAR-100部分类别删除任务上，局部教师蒸馏显著缩小了遗忘模型与重训练模型的预测差异（尤其在遗忘集附近），同时保持了有竞争力的聚合遗忘指标。

Conclusion: 局部连带遗忘是机器遗忘中的一个重要失败模式，局部教师蒸馏作为一种简单有效的缓解策略，能更好地逼近重训练基准，提示未来遗忘评估应关注局部而非仅聚合指标。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of selected training examples without full retraining. Standard evaluations often summarize unlearning quality with aggregate metrics, such as accuracy- and forgetting-based scores, which can hide localized failures. We study this failure mode at the example level by comparing the predictions of an unlearned model to those of the model retrained after deletion. We show that this pointwise discrepancy can be highly non-uniform: for gradient-ascent and random-labeling methods, with and without retain-set fine-tuning, it grows with geometric proximity to the forget set. We call this phenomenon localized collateral forgetting. Our analysis identifies a mechanism behind the effect: surrogate targets used during unlearning can be inconsistent with the local prediction structure induced by retraining, and this inconsistency propagates through shared representations to nearby examples. Motivated by this mechanism, we propose Local Teacher Distillation, a simple mitigation strategy that replaces random targets with soft labels from a small teacher trained only on retained neighbors of the forget set. On CIFAR-100 partial-class deletion, this local teacher brings the unlearned model substantially closer to retraining, especially near the forget set, while maintaining competitive aggregate unlearning metrics.

</details>


### [204] [Generalized Intention Modeling in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31318)
*Mateusz Odrowaz-Sypniewski,Jasmine Bayrooti,Ajay Shankar,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种任务自适应的对手建模框架，通过性能驱动的多意图表示混合与最大化与自身未来回报互信息的新意图表示方法，在多种非合作多智能体强化学习任务中达到或超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手建模方法预先选定某类信息（如对手下一动作或未来环境状态）作为意图表示，但这些信息的代表性依赖于具体任务和环境，缺乏通用性，导致模型在不同任务上表现不稳定。

Method: 提出任务自适应对手建模框架：(1) 学习一个性能驱动的多意图表示混合机制；(2) 引入一种新的意图表示，最大化与自身未来回报之间的互信息，从而捕获与性能最直接相关的对手信息。

Result: 在多种不同任务上一致地达到或超越现有最优基线方法，并揭示了不同对手建模策略何时以及为何有效。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的多意图混合表示能更好地捕捉通用意图，且最大化与未来回报互信息的表示方法更直接地服务于智能体的决策性能。

Abstract: Modeling an opponent's intent is critical for effective decision-making in non-cooperative, competitive, and general-sum multi-agent reinforcement learning. Existing opponent modeling methods encode intent using an embedding derived from episode information chosen a priori, such as the opponent's next action or a future environment state, and use this to guide the ego-agent's behavior. These approaches assume that the chosen information is universally representative of intent; however, we show empirically that this is not the case as intentions are often task- and environment-dependent. To address this, we introduce a task-adaptive opponent modeling framework that learns a performance-driven mixture of multiple intent representations. We further introduce a new intention representation that maximizes mutual information with the ego-agent's future returns, thereby capturing opponent information that is most directly relevant to performance. Our approach consistently matches or exceeds the performance of state-of-the-art baselines across diverse tasks and yields insights into when and why different opponent modeling strategies succeed.

</details>


### [205] [A Unifying View of Variational Generative Wasserstein Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31369)
*Paul Caucheteux,Clément Bonet,Anna Korba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了生成Wasserstein流（GWF）的统一理论框架，证明多种现有生成模型可视为参数化JKO格式的实例，并将该框架扩展到IPM和MMD等散度，推导出新的生成算法，最后分析了参数化Wasserstein流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型虽可视为最小化概率分布散度，但依赖不同的算法和几何原理，缺乏统一的视角。作者希望建立一个基于Wasserstein梯度流的统一理论框架，以揭示和连接这些方法。

Method: 1. 将生成模型建立为Wasserstein梯度流的参数化JKO格式；2. 证明多种现有方法（包括f-散度目标）是该框架的特例；3. 将框架扩展到IPM和squared MMD，推导新算法；4. 通过实验研究JKO正则化的影响；5. 分析参数化Wasserstein流（动力学限制于参数化映射诱导的分布）。

Result: 建立了生成模型的统一理论框架，揭示了多种算法之间的等价关系，推导了基于JKO的新生成算法，并实证研究了JKO正则化在不同目标上的效果。

Conclusion: Wasserstein梯度流为生成模型提供了统一的理论基础，JKO格式不仅连接了现有方法，还能导出新算法，参数化Wasserstein流的分析为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Many modern generative models can be viewed as minimizing divergences between probability distributions, yet they rely on different algorithmic and geometric principles. Wasserstein gradient flows provide a continuous-time formulation for optimizing over distributions, and can be approximated through their implicit discretization via the Jordan-Kinderlehrer-Otto (JKO) scheme. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for generative modeling based on Wasserstein gradient flows, which we refer to as Generative Wasserstein Flows (GWF). We show that a broad class of existing methods can be derived as instances of parametric JKO schemes for $f$-divergence objectives, and we establish equivalences between several recently proposed algorithms. We extend this framework beyond f-divergence to Integral Probability Metrics and squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy, deriving new JKO-based generative algorithms, and clarifying their connections with GANs. We study empirically the impact of the JKO regularization for a wide set of objectives. Finally, we analyze parametric Wasserstein flows, where the dynamics are restricted to distributions induced by parametrized maps.

</details>


### [206] [Softsign: Smooth Sign in Your Optimizer For Better Parameter Heterogeneity Handling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31371)
*Dmitrii Feoktistov,Timofey Belinsky,Andrey Veprikov,Amir Zainullin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SoftSignum和SoftMuon优化器，用温度控制的软符号变换替代硬符号映射，结合自适应分位数温度调度，在保持低内存优势的同时改善收敛性能，在多种深度学习任务（含LLM预训练）上超越硬符号优化器和AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于符号和LMO的优化器因固定幅度更新导致终端收敛差：更新机制与梯度幅度解耦，忽略参数异质性，易产生振荡而非收敛。

Method: 1) 用温度控制的soft-sign变换替代hard sign，实现从符号更新到SGD类幅度敏感更新的参数级平滑过渡；2) 自适应分位数温度调度；3) 将相同原理扩展到矩阵值优化器得到SoftMuon；4) 基于强凸正则化和Fenchel共轭的通用几何松弛框架，证明非凸随机收敛性。

Result: 在多种深度学习任务（包括LLM预训练）上，SoftSignum和SoftMuon持续优于硬符号优化器和标准AdamW。

Conclusion: 所提出的软符号松弛方法和几何松弛框架有效改善了符号优化器的终端收敛问题，在保持低内存优势的同时实现了更优性能。

Abstract: Sign-based and LMO-inspired optimizers have recently attracted substantial attention in deep learning due to their strong performance and low memory footprint. However, their fixed-magnitude updates can hurt terminal convergence: they decouple update mechanisms from gradient magnitudes and fail to account for parameter heterogeneity, often leading to oscillation rather than convergence. We propose SoftSignum, a smooth relaxation of sign-based optimization that replaces the hard sign map with a temperature-controlled soft-sign transformation, enabling a parameter-wise transition from sign-like updates to magnitude-sensitive SGD-like steps. We complement it with an adaptive quantile-based temperature schedule and extend the same principle to matrix-valued optimizers, obtaining SoftMuon. We also develop a generalized geometry-relaxation framework based on strongly convex regularizers and Fenchel conjugates, proving convergence in stochastic non-convex setting. Experiments on diverse deep learning tasks, including LLM pretraining, show that SoftSignum and SoftMuon consistently improve over their hard sign-based counterparts and standard AdamW.

</details>


### [207] [Constrained Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Max-Min Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31388)
*Giseung Park,Hyunyoung Nam,Woohyeon Byeon,Amir Leshem,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合最大最小准则与显式约束满足的多目标强化学习框架，通过理论分析、收敛性证明及表格环境实验验证了其有效性，并在建筑热控制、多目标运动控制和温室气体排放感知交通管理等实际场景中展示了其在平衡公平性与约束满足方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最大最小MORL方法在需要显式约束满足的场景下适用性有限，缺乏同时处理公平性目标和约束条件的统一框架。

Method: 提出一个集成最大最小准则与显式约束满足的MORL框架，建立理论基础，并在表格设置下进行收敛性分析与实验验证。

Result: 在模拟建筑热控制、多目标运动控制以及温室气体排放感知交通管理三个领域验证了该方法在平衡公平性与约束满足方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效融合公平性目标与约束满足，拓展了MORL在约束感知场景下的应用能力。

Abstract: Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) extends standard RL by optimizing policies with respect to multiple, often conflicting, objectives. While max-min MORL has emerged as an effective approach for promoting fairness, its applicability remains limited, particularly when constraints must be incorporated. In this paper, we propose a MORL framework that integrates the max-min criterion with explicit constraint satisfaction. We establish a theoretical foundation for the proposed framework and validate the resulting algorithm through convergence analysis and experiments in tabular settings. We further demonstrate the practical relevance of our approach in simulated building thermal control, multi-objective locomotion control, and greenhouse-gas-emission-aware traffic management. Across these domains, our method effectively balances fairness and constraint satisfaction in multi-objective decision-making.

</details>


### [208] [Fixed Universal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31423)
*Jingwen Liu,Alexandr Andoni,Daniel Hsu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"通用Transformer"概念——固定权重的Transformer通过输入嵌入即可模拟同类中的任意Transformer，证明了大维度下的稀疏构造和随机初始化的通用性，实验验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer的表达能力是否主要来自输入表示而非学习的权重，类比通用图灵机，检验固定参数Transformer能否通过输入编码模拟任意目标模型。

Method: 提出通用Transformer框架，给出显式稀疏构造（嵌入维度足够大时保证通用性），并证明随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然具有通用性，结合括号平衡与多跳推理任务进行经验验证。

Result: 理论上证明了充分大嵌入维度下稀疏构造可实现通用性，且随机初始化的Transformer几乎必然通用；实验上在括号平衡和多跳推理任务中验证了理论。

Conclusion: Transformer的表达能力很大程度上源于输入表示而非学习到的权重参数。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{universal transformers}: fixed transformers that can simulate any transformer in a given class via a suitable input embedding. Analogous to a universal Turing machine, the input embedding encodes a description of the target model while all internal parameters remain fixed. We provide explicit sparse constructions achieving universality when the embedding dimension is sufficiently large, and further show that universality is generic: randomly initialized transformers are universal almost surely, which aligns with recent empirical results of Zhong and Andreas (2024). We empirically validate our theory on the algorithmic tasks of parenthesis balancing and multi-hop reasoning. Our results suggest that much of a transformer's expressive power may reside in its input representation rather than its learned weights.

</details>


### [209] [DG-CoLearn: An Efficient Collaborative Learning Framework for Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31427)
*Ashley Hoi-Ting Au,Zikun Zhang,Ligang He,Qiang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DG-CoLearn提出了一种基于增量图快照处理的无感知客户协作动态图学习框架，通过服务器中介的嵌入交换机制实现跨客户端消息传递，在保护结构隐私的同时，训练速度提升最高33.8倍，通信开销降低最高27.4倍，节点分类F1提升13.36%，链接预测MAP提升8.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态图学习方法因重复的全快照重训练导致计算开销大，且不适合数据分区的协作场景；跨分区边不可避免，但直接共享图结构违反隐私约束。

Method: 提出增量图快照处理机制，仅计算受时间更新影响的图区域，并通过时间建模保留历史信息；引入服务器中介的嵌入交换机制，在不暴露原始跨客户端结构信息的前提下实现准确的多跳消息传递。

Result: 训练时间加速最高33.8倍，通信开销降低最高27.4倍；节点分类F1提升最高13.36%，链接预测MAP提升最高8.27%。

Conclusion: DG-CoLearn在效率、可扩展性和客户端间结构隐私之间实现了有效平衡，验证了增量处理在协作动态图学习中的优越性。

Abstract: Dynamic graph learning (DGL) is essential for modelling evolving graph data, but existing methods suffer from significant computational overhead due to repeated full-snapshot retraining and are not well-suited for collaborative settings with partitioned data. In realistic graph systems, cross-partition edges are unavoidable, but direct sharing of graph structure between clients may violate privacy constraints. We propose DG-CoLearn, a client-oblivious collaborative dynamic graph learning framework built on incremental graph snapshot processing, which focuses computation on graph regions affected by temporal updates while preserving historical information through temporal modelling. This incremental design is consistently applied across the entire graph processing pipeline, including a server-mediated embedding exchange mechanism to enable accurate multi-hop message passing without exposing raw cross-client structural information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DG-CoLearn achieves up to 33.8$\times$ speedup in training time and 27.4$\times$ reduction in communication overhead, while consistently improving predictive performance on both node classification (up to 13.36% F1 improvement) and link prediction (up to 8.27% MAP improvement) tasks. These results highlight the effectiveness of DG-CoLearn in bridging efficiency, scalability, and client-to-client structural privacy in collaborative dynamic graph learning.

</details>


### [210] [Flow map learning in nonlinear vector autoregressive models: influence of the feature-library structure on the training error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31438)
*Markus Gross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性向量自回归（NVAR/NG-RC）模型在时间序列预测中的可辨识性问题，揭示了训练误差随时间分辨率变化的（预）渐近标度律，并指出特征库能否精确表示流映射的李级数系数决定了这些定律。研究发现，延迟项虽能降低单步训练误差，但仅当特征库提供足够非线性度时才能改善长期预测；模型类与数据生成过程不匹配时，小训练误差与弱泛化共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测通常需要学习非线性和时滞依赖关系，但现有非线性向量自回归（NVAR）模型（即下一代储层计算NG-RC）的可辨识性尚不清晰，尤其是训练误差与时间分辨率之间的标度关系缺乏理论理解。

Method: 从Koopman算子近似视角出发，分析NVAR/NG-RC模型在马尔可夫非线性动力系统上的可辨识性；推导训练误差关于时间分辨率的（预）渐近标度律；利用单特征项和傅里叶特征库对多项式向量场系统进行理论分析，并通过数值实验在多个混沌动力系统上验证。

Result: 训练误差随时间分辨率遵循特征标度律，该定律依赖于特征库能否精确表示流映射的早期李级数系数；延迟项能降低最优单步训练误差，但仅当特征库提供足够非线性度时才能改善长时域预测；模型类与真实数据生成过程不匹配时，小训练误差不保证强泛化能力。

Conclusion: NVAR/NG-RC模型的泛化能力不仅取决于误差最小化，更取决于特征库与数据生成动力系统的结构匹配程度；延迟项的作用具有条件性，单纯增加延迟无法补偿特征库非线性度的不足。

Abstract: Time series forecasting often requires learning nonlinear and time-delayed dependencies. A paradigmatic class of forecasting models are nonlinear vector autoregressive processes (NVAR), also known as next-generation reservoir computers (NG-RCs). These models approximate the Koopman operator on the space spanned by their explicit feature library. We consider the identifiability problem for learning Markovian nonlinear dynamical systems and show that the training error as a function of time resolution follows characteristic (pre-)asymptotic scaling laws. These laws depend on whether the feature library can represent the early Lie-series coefficients of the flow map (propagator) exactly or merely approximately. For dynamical systems governed by polynomial vector fields, we demonstrate the mechanism for NVAR/NG-RC models with monomial and Fourier feature libraries. We determine the dependence of the training error on the temporal resolution, the involved nonlinear degree, and the number of delay terms. While delay terms reduce the optimal one-step training error, they improve long-horizon forecasts only when the library provides sufficient nonlinearity. Thus, small training error coexists with weak generalization as the model class is mismatched to the true data-generating process. Numerical experiments on various chaotic dynamical systems confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [211] [Balanced LoRA: Removing Parameter Invariance to Accelerate Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31484)
*Valérie Castin,Kimia Nadjahi,Pierre Ablin,Gabriel Peyré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BaLoRA，通过对LoRA的低秩因子进行平衡投影来改善损失景观的条件数，从而加快收敛速度并提升微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA存在过参数化问题，不同低秩因子对的条件数差异显著，影响收敛速率。现有方法未充分关注这一几何特性对优化动态的影响。

Method: 提出BaLoRA，在每次迭代后将低秩因子投影到平衡流形上，改善损失景观的条件数，同时保持适配矩阵不变。投影步骤计算轻量，可无缝集成到现有微调流水线中。

Result: 理论与实验均表明，BaLoRA比标准LoRA收敛更快，在多种微调任务上取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 通过平衡低秩因子的条件数可以有效加速LoRA的收敛，BaLoRA是一种简单有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is the most widely adopted method for fine-tuning large language models. Notably, LoRA is inherently overparameterized: multiple pairs of low-rank factors can yield the same adapted weight matrix. We show--both theoretically and empirically--that these pairs exhibit significantly different condition numbers. As a result, converging to different loss minimizers directly impacts the convergence rate of LoRA. Building on this observation, we introduce Balanced Low-Rank Adaptation (BaLoRA), a variant of LoRA that projects iterates onto a balanced manifold. This manifold improves the conditioning of the loss landscape while preserving the adapted matrix. The projection step is computationally lightweight and integrates seamlessly into existing fine-tuning pipelines. Empirically, BaLoRA converges faster than standard LoRA and achieves superior performance across a range of fine-tuning tasks.

</details>


### [212] [Assign and Add: A Mechanistic Study of Compositional Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31497)
*Brady Exoo,Alberto Bietti,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过变量赋值和模加法任务，研究了Transformer中组合泛化的机制，发现模型复用了相同的模块化加法MLP模块，训练呈现三阶段动力学，并提供了理论框架解释组合性如何从训练动态中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer如何组合已有技能以执行复杂任务（尤其是训练中未见的组合）的内在机制。

Method: 在简化的受控环境（变量赋值+模加法）中，将训练数据划分为不相交集合，通过机械论分析（mechanistic analysis）追踪内部模块的使用模式，并结合训练动力学实证观察。

Result: 小型Transformer能够泛化到未见过的变量与数字组合；相同的“模加法”MLP模块在直接输入和间接变量赋值场景下被复用；训练呈现三阶段：学习模加法→学习变量赋值结构→针对硬序列的精炼阶段。

Conclusion: 组合泛化可以是Transformer内部机制组合性的自然结果，为理解组合涌现提供了理论与实证基础。

Abstract: Large language models are able to compose skills in order to perform complex tasks, many of which might not have been seen during training. The details of how exactly this composition occurs remain elusive. In this paper, we study a mechanism for compositional generalization in transformers by considering a simple controlled setting involving variable assignment and modular addition. By partitioning our training data into disjoint sets, we observe that small transformers are able to generalize to previously unseen combinations of variables and numbers. Our mechanistic analysis shows that the same ``modular addition'' MLP module is used whether the inputs are given directly or indirectly through a separate variable assignment mechanism. We also analyze the training dynamics from an empirical lens, which reveals three phases of learning: first, modular addition is learned, then the structure required for variable assignment, and finally a refinement phase where the model generalizes to some hard sequences not seen in training. Finally, we provide a theoretical framework to explain how compositionality emerges from training dynamics. These results suggest that compositional generalization can be a natural consequence of the compositionality of internal mechanisms in~transformers.

</details>


### [213] [Scalable Inference-Time Annealing with Surrogate Likelihood Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31498)
*Daniel Peñaherrera,Rishal Aggarwal,David Ryan Koes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SITA方法，通过重训练流模型并利用基于能量的模型作为快速替代似然，在推理时沿温度阶梯迭代微调生成模型，避免昂贵的散度计算，在丙氨酸二肽和三肽上实现最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统采样方法计算成本高，而现有基于扩散模型的退火方法在推理时需计算分数场的散度来估计重要性权重，对于大分子体系计算量过大甚至不可行。

Method: SITA沿温度阶梯重训练基于流的生成模型，使用基于能量的模型（EBM）提供快速替代似然，避免直接计算分数场的散度项。

Result: 在丙氨酸二肽和丙氨酸三肽上取得最先进性能，且避免了昂贵的散度计算开销。

Conclusion: SITA是一种可扩展的推理时退火方法，在不增加计算复杂度的前提下有效提升分子构象采样效率，适用于更大分子体系。

Abstract: A long standing challenge in computational chemistry and biophysics is efficiently sampling the Boltzmann distribution of molecules. Advances in generative modeling have been proposed to address the limitations of conventional sampling techniques by eliminating the computational cost of simulation. A promising direction is iteratively finetuning diffusion models along a temperature ladder whereby training data is generated via importance sampling during inference-time annealing. Unfortunately, these methods require computing a divergence over the score field to estimate importance weights, rendering them intractable for larger systems. Here we present scalable inference-time annealing (SITA), which retrains flow-based models to generate samples at progressively lower temperatures using an energy-based model to facilitate fast surrogate likelihoods. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on both Alanine Dipeptide and Alanine Tripeptide while avoiding costly divergence terms. Our code is available at: https://github.com/countrsignal/sita.git

</details>


### [214] [On the Relationship Between Activation Outliers and Feature Death in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31518)
*Elana Simon,Etowah Adams,James Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）中存在特征死亡问题，由激活值的维度级离群值导致，其严重程度可由γ=‖μ‖/‖σ‖量化。均值中心化预处理可消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE学习到的特征中存在特征死亡问题，浪费字典容量并可能重新引入叠加现象，且不同模型间死亡率的巨大差异缺乏合理解释。

Method: 提出维度级激活离群值的概念，形式化为γ=‖μ‖/‖σ‖；通过分析预激活与激活均值的对齐关系解释死亡机制；提出均值中心化作为预处理步骤。

Result: γ可预测初始死亡率（Spearman ρ=0.89 for dead-by-TopK, 0.82 for dead-by-ReLU），覆盖454个模型-层组合；均值中心化可消除所有测试模型中的离群值诱导死亡。

Conclusion: 特征死亡的根本原因是激活均值导致的预激活偏移，均值中心化是解决该问题的有效且原则性的预处理手段。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose neural network activations into interpretable features, but many learned features never activate, a problem called feature death that wastes dictionary capacity and can reintroduce superposition. Death rates vary dramatically between models: near-zero on GPT-2, over 70% on AlphaFold3 with identical configurations. We find that dimension-level activation outliers (dimensions whose mean magnitude is large relative to per-token variation) cause this by shifting pre-activations at initialization based on each feature's alignment with the activation mean. Features anti-aligned with the mean receive permanently negative pre-activations and never fire. We formalize outlier severity as $γ= \|μ\|/\|σ\|$; it predicts initial death rates (Spearman $ρ= 0.89$ for dead-by-TopK, $0.82$ for dead-by-ReLU) across 454 model-layer combinations spanning language, vision, protein, and genomic models. Dead features can revive during training, but recovery requires the SAE bias to learn the activation mean, a process that is prohibitively slow at high $γ$. Mean-centering (subtracting the activation mean) sidesteps this and eliminates outlier-induced death across all tested models, confirming the mechanism and providing a principled basis for when and why this preprocessing step is necessary.

</details>


### [215] [Chem-PerturBridge: a harmonized compendium of small molecule perturbation transcriptomic effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31522)
*Artur Szałata,Olga Novitskaia,Maiia Shulman,Matthew Mella,Altynbek Zhubanchaliyev,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chem-PerturBridge是一个整合了37K+化合物、136种细胞环境和125万转录组样本的多数据集资源，涵盖8种分析类型。研究发现不同数据集间相同化合物的logFC排序一致性较弱，但方向一致性较好。该资源作为预训练数据能显著提升化合物表示学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小分子转录组资源分散在不同技术平台、元数据规范、对照、剂量和预处理流程之间，缺乏统一的大规模训练数据来支持需要考虑化学、细胞和分析多样性的扰动模型。

Method: 构建Chem-PerturBridge：整合37K+化合物、136种细胞环境、1.25M转录组样本、8种分析类型，统一标识符、元数据并计算可重复感知的条件级效应。评估跨数据集匹配条件一致性和数据集内重复一致性，并作为预训练资源用于化合物表示学习。

Result: 跨数据集相同化合物条件在细粒度logFC排序和幅值上一致性较弱（常低于同环境不同化合物基线），但logFC方向一致性更稳定且超过基线。预训练嵌入在OP3评估和11个数据集的分子留出评估中优于L1000-only嵌入、Morgan指纹和OP3基线。

Conclusion: Chem-PerturBridge既支持跨数据集签名一致性的诊断评估，也支持异质扰动转录组数据的模型导向复用。

Abstract: Large perturbation models require training data encompassing chemical, cellular, and assay diversity. Current transcriptomic resources for small-molecule modeling, however, are fragmented across technologies, metadata conventions, controls, doses, and preprocessing pipelines. We introduce Chem-PerturBridge, a harmonized multi-dataset resource comprising over 37k compounds, 136 cellular contexts, and 1.25M transcriptomic samples across eight assay types, with standardized identifiers, metadata, and replicate-aware condition-level effects. We use the resource to evaluate matched-condition agreement across datasets and replicate agreement within datasets. Matched same-compound conditions generally show weak agreement in fine-grained logFC rankings and magnitudes across most dataset pairs, often falling below same-context different-compound baselines. In contrast, logFC direction agreement is substantially more stable and usually exceeds these baselines. We further evaluate Chem-PerturBridge as a pretraining resource for compound representation learning. Under a compound-held-out OP3 evaluation split, embeddings pretrained on Chem-PerturBridge improve over L1000-only embeddings, Morgan fingerprints, and the descriptor-free OP3 baseline across metrics. An extensive molecule-holdout evaluation across 11 datasets further shows that models trained on Chem-PerturBridge outperform or match those that are not. Chem-PerturBridge therefore supports both diagnostic evaluation of cross-dataset signature agreement and model-oriented reuse of heterogeneous perturbation transcriptomic data.

</details>


### [216] [Value Functions as Supermartingale Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31524)
*Alessandro Abate,Daniel Contro,Mirco Giacobbe,Agustín Martínez-Suñé,Diptarko Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了基于超鞅证书的随机系统形式化验证与强化学习（RL）之间的理论联系，证明了在适当奖励函数下，几乎必然满足ω-正则性质的策略所对应的值函数编码了一个Streett超鞅证书，从而为通过RL进行证书合成提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认证方法（基于超鞅证书）能为随机系统提供ω-正则性质（包括LTL）的几乎必然满足的形式化证明，但这些方法需要手动构造证书；而RL方法虽能学习ω-正则任务策略，但缺乏形式化保证。两类研究之间存在鸿沟，亟需建立理论桥梁以结合两者的优势。

Method: 在理论上证明了在适当奖励函数设计下，几乎必然满足ω-正则性质的策略的价值函数对应一个Streett超鞅证书；并在有限MDP上进行了实验验证。

Result: 建立了价值函数与Streett超鞅证书之间的理论等价关系，该结果对有限、可数无限和连续状态空间均成立，并在有限MDP上得到实验验证。

Conclusion: 该理论连接为通过RL方法自动合成形式化验证证书提供了一条原则性路径，使得RL不仅能学习满足规格的策略，还能同时获得该策略满足规格的形式化保证。

Abstract: Certification methods for stochastic systems provide sufficient proof rules, based on real-valued supermartingale certificates, to determine the almost-sure satisfaction of $ω$-regular properties (and therefore of linear temporal logic) over general state spaces, encompassing both countably infinite and continuous state spaces. Conversely, reinforcement learning (RL) methods for $ω$-regular tasks have received considerable attention, but they typically lack formal guarantees that the learned policy satisfies the specification, except possibly for finite state and action spaces. We bridge these two lines of research by establishing a novel theoretical connection: under an appropriate reward, the value function associated to a policy that almost surely satisfies an $ω$-regular property encodes a Streett supermartingale certificate for that specification. Our results, validated experimentally on finite Markov decision processes, hold for finite, countably infinite, and continuous state spaces, suggesting a principled route to certificate synthesis via RL.

</details>


### [217] [The Dynamic-Probabilistic Consistency Gap in Chaotic Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31547)
*Andre Herz,Matthijs Pals,Daniel Durstewitz,Georgia Koppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动态系统重建中的动态-概率一致性（DPC）鸿沟：有限时域概率目标会恶化动力学或使预测不确定性偏离局部切线动力学。提出了KAFFEE（基于可微扩展卡尔曼滤波的遍历仿真框架），通过在局部预测残差上评估似然度并利用学习到的局部雅可比矩阵传输协方差，有效缓解了核心坍缩、噪声掩盖和盲不确定性三种失效模式，在随机超混沌Lorenz-96和13个混沌系统上提升了动力学不变量的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态系统重建(DSR)方法在追求有限时域概率目标时，会产生动态-概率一致性(DPC)鸿沟：要么退化系统动力学，要么使预测不确定性偏离其应反映的局部切线动力学。需要一种能够同时保持动力学保真度和概率校准一致性的训练框架。

Method: 提出KAFFEE框架：基于可微扩展卡尔曼滤波(EKF)的训练方法，在局部预测残差（创新）上评估似然度，同时通过学习到的局部雅可比矩阵传输协方差，从而将不确定性估计与系统局部切线动力学对齐。

Result: 在随机超混沌Lorenz-96上，KAFFEE减少了核心坍缩、噪声掩盖和盲不确定性三种失效模式，改善了动力学不变量重建，保持竞争性预测分数。在13个混沌系统上的DSR基础模型概率适配实验中，KAFFEE实现了上下文贝叶斯滤波同时基本保持零样本动力学。

Conclusion: DPC鸿沟是动态系统概率重建中的一个关键问题，KAFFEE通过局部似然与雅可比协方差传输的结合，有效弥合了这一鸿沟，为动态系统的不确定性感知重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dynamical systems reconstruction (DSR) aims to learn surrogate models that capture the dynamics underlying time-series data. Reliably deploying these surrogates requires uncertainty estimates consistent with the learned dynamics. We expose a dynamic-probabilistic consistency (DPC) gap: the pursuit of finite-horizon probabilistic objectives can degrade dynamics or decouple predictive uncertainty from the local tangent dynamics it ought to reflect. We isolate three mechanisms behind this gap: core collapse, noise masking, and blind uncertainty. Specifically, we show that open-loop Gaussian rollout objectives can penalize Jacobian-generated covariance growth in chaotic systems, encouraging optimization shortcuts that weaken physical expansion or decouple uncertainty from it. To mitigate this gap, we propose KAFFEE (Kalman-Aware Framework For Ergodic Emulation), a differentiable extended Kalman filter-based training framework that evaluates likelihood on local predictive residuals (innovations) while transporting covariance through learned local Jacobians. On stochastic hyperchaotic Lorenz-96, KAFFEE reduces the identified failure modes, improves reconstruction of dynamical invariants relative to open-loop objectives, and maintains competitive predictive scores. We further show that the DPC gap appears when probabilistically adapting a DSR foundation model across 13 chaotic systems, where KAFFEE enables in-context Bayesian filtering while largely preserving zero-shot dynamics.

</details>


### [218] [Functional Attention: From Pairwise Affinities to Functional Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31559)
*Jiefang Xiao,Maolin Gao,Simon Weber,Guandao Yang,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“Functional Attention”的算子学习方法，将注意力机制重新解释为自适应基函数之间的泛函对应，通过结构化线性算子替代softmax亲和度，实现了紧凑、可泛化、分辨率不变的全局依赖建模，在PDE求解、3D分割和回归等任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的算子学习方法通常将连续场视为离散token进行逐点注意力计算，忽略了全局功能结构，难以捕捉函数空间中的全局依赖关系。为此，受几何泛函映射启发，作者希望设计一种能够显式捕获全局依赖、具备分辨率不变性的算子学习机制。

Method: 将注意力机制重新解释为自适应基函数之间的泛函对应（Functional Correspondence），用结构化线性算子替代传统的softmax亲和度矩阵，从而将离散的token-wise注意力转化为连续函数空间中的算子映射，实现紧凑且分辨率不变的表示。

Result: 在多个算子学习任务（包括PDE求解、3D分割和回归）中，该方法达到了与最先进方法相当的性能，同时对不同的离散化方式表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Functional Attention通过泛函视角重新设计注意力机制，有效克服了传统token-wise注意力忽略全局功能结构的局限，为算子学习提供了一种更紧凑、可泛化且分辨率不变的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional function spaces, or operator learning, is essential for many machine learning applications. Although transformer-based operators are popular, they often rely on token-wise attention. These methods treat continuous fields as discrete tokens and usually ignore the global functional structure. We introduce \emph{Functional Attention}, which reinterprets attention as a functional correspondence between adaptive bases. Inspired by geometric functional maps, our method replaces softmax affinities with structured linear operators. This yields a compact, generalizable, resolution-invariant representation that explicitly captures global dependencies. Experiments demonstrate that \emph{Functional Attention} can match state-of-the-art performance in many operator learning tasks, including solving PDEs, 3D segmentation, and regression, while remaining robust to varying discretizations. Project page is available at https://github.com/xjffff/FUNCATTN.

</details>


### [219] [Effective Biological Representation Learning by Masking Gene Expression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31562)
*Kian Kenyon-Dean,Alina Selega,Ihab Bendidi,Jordan M. Sorokin,Luca Bertinetto,David Errington,Hayley Donnella,Oren Kraus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自监督转录组基础模型TxFM，通过针对RNA-seq计数数据的掩码自编码方法，结合精心筛选的DiverseRNA-1.4M训练语料库，在归纳表示学习评估中显著优于线性基线和超大规模模型，证明精心设计的自监督学习策略在转录组学中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有转录组基础模型在线性基线面前表现不佳，引发深度表示学习相较于原始转录本计数是否具有优势的质疑。本文旨在探索自监督表示学习能否为转录组学提供真正的性能优势。

Method: 提出TxFM模型，采用针对多样化RNA-seq计数数据定制的掩码自编码方法；进行消融实验识别关键架构配置；构建DiverseRNA-1.4M公开训练语料库。

Result: TxFM在归纳表示学习评估中生成高保真基因表示；在DiverseRNA-1.4M上训练的模型性能优于使用超100倍规模数据训练的模型；消融实验确认了关键架构配置的重要性。

Conclusion: 归纳式自监督学习是转录组学表示学习的可行方法，关键在于模型架构与训练数据筛选的精心协同设计。

Abstract: RNA sequencing produces rich and diverse datasets of gene expression, offering compelling insights into cellular state and function that have many applications in drug discovery. Modeling such data is challenging due to inherent technical noise and experimental batch effects, as evidenced by many existing transcriptomic foundation models (FMs) underperforming relative to linear baselines. Such results raise the question of whether deep representation learning provides a distinct advantage over the direct use of raw transcript counts. Our work explores this by developing a new self-supervised model, TxFM, with a focus on inductive representation learning evaluations. TxFM employs a masked autoencoding approach tailored to diverse RNA-seq count data, and our ablation study empirically identifies crucial architecture configurations required for strong transfer performance. Additionally, we curate a public training corpus, DiverseRNA-1.4M, and find that TxFM trained on this curated dataset yields high-fidelity gene representations that outperform FMs trained on atlas-scale corpora over 100x larger. Overall, our results indicate that inductive self-supervised learning is a viable modeling approach for transcriptomics representation, provided a careful synthesis of model architecture and training data curation.

</details>


### [220] [Giving Sensors a Voice: Multimodal JEPA for Semantic Time-Series Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31580)
*Utsav Dutta,Gerardo Pastrana,Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad,Henrik Ohlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CHARM模型，利用通道级文本描述和JEPA架构，在异构多元时间序列上学习通用表征，通过线性探测在异常检测、分类和预测任务上取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构在语言和视觉领域的序列建模取得了显著进展，但针对异构多元时间序列的通用表征学习仍未得到充分探索。

Method: CHARM将通道级文本描述融入对通道顺序等变的Transformer编码器，采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）和新的损失函数来促进信息丰富且时间稳定的嵌入；通过潜在空间预测增强对传感器噪声的鲁棒性，描述感知门控机制提供跨通道关系的可解释性。

Result: 在异常检测、分类、短期和长期预测任务上，学习到的嵌入仅使用线性探测即取得强性能。

Conclusion: 性能主要归因于JEPA目标和条件架构，文本描述作为跨数据集泛化的通道标识符。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have advanced sequence modeling in language and vision, yet general-purpose representation learning for heterogeneous multivariate time series remains underexplored. We introduce CHARM (Channel-Aware Representation Model), which incorporates channel-level textual descriptions into a Transformer encoder equivariant to channel order. CHARM is trained with a Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) and a novel loss promoting informative, temporally stable embeddings; latent-space prediction encourages robustness to sensor noise while description-aware gating provides interpretability through learned inter-channel relationships. Across anomaly detection, classification, and short- and long-term forecasting, the learned embeddings achieve strong performance using only a linear probe. Performance is driven primarily by the JEPA objective and conditioning architecture, with text descriptions serving as channel identifiers for cross-dataset generalization.

</details>


### [221] [A Tight Theory of Error Feedback Algorithms in Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31594)
*Daniel Berg Thomsen,Adrien Taylor,Aymeric Dieuleveut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式学习中的两种主流误差反馈压缩算法（EF和EF21）进行了紧致收敛性分析，通过识别最优步长和构造最优Lyapunov函数，证明了其收敛保证与智能体数量无关，且恢复单智能体场景下的已知最优界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习和一阶优化中通信成本是主要瓶颈，梯度压缩虽能缓解但会降低收敛保证。误差反馈机制可简单高效地解决此问题，但众多变体的相对性能尚未被充分理解，亟需理论上的系统对比分析。

Method: 针对经典误差反馈方法（EF）和EF21两种算法，通过识别最优步长选择并构造专属于每种方法的最优Lyapunov函数，进行紧致（tight）收敛性分析。

Result: 分析结果与智能体数量无关，且恢复单智能体场景下已知的最优收敛保证，表明两种方法在多智能体场景下均能保持理论最优性。

Conclusion: 本文为EF和EF21提供了紧致的收敛理论，明确了最优步长和Lyapunov函数的设计，有助于指导实际分布式学习中误差反馈算法的选择与调参。

Abstract: Communication costs are a major bottleneck in distributed learning and first-order optimization. A common approach to alleviate this issue is to compress the gradient information exchanged between agents. However, such compression typically degrades the convergence guarantees of gradient-based methods. Error feedback mechanisms provide a simple and computationally cheap remedy for this issue, but numerous variants have been proposed, and their relative performance remains poorly understood. This paper provides tight convergence analyses for two of the main error-feedback algorithms from the literature, the classic Error Feedback method (EF) and Error Feedback 21 (EF21), by identifying optimal step-size choices and constructing optimal Lyapunov functions tailored to each method. The results hold independently of the number of agents and recover the known best guarantees possible in the single-agent regime.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [PhyDrawGen: Physically Grounded Diagram Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30512)
*Nafiul Haque,Syed Nazmus Sakib,Shifat E Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhyDrawGen是一个神经符号管道，通过LLM提取场景图、确定性求解器生成几何图元、视觉模型迭代校正约束违规，在1449个物理问题基准上显著优于GPT-5-image等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在物理图中存在力矢量幻觉、忽略守恒定律、违反几何约束等问题，需要严格遵循物理定律的图表生成方法。

Method: 1) LLM从问题文本提取带类型的场景图；2) 确定性求解器将场景图转换为Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG)，编码力平衡、光路和场拓扑为精确几何基元；3) 微调Qwen-VL模型通过视觉定位的提议-验证循环迭代修正约束违反。

Result: 在涵盖力学、光学和电磁学的1449个问题基准上，PhyDrawGen显著优于GPT-5-image、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 3 Pro，在非常规物体问题上也表现出鲁棒的物理准确性。

Conclusion: 将语义理解与物理约束满足解耦的神经符号方法能有效提升物理图生成的准确性和物理一致性。

Abstract: Generating physics diagrams from text requires strict adherence to physical laws. While current generative models produce visually plausible outputs, they systematically hallucinate force vectors, ignore conservation laws, and violate geometric constraints. We present PhyDrawGen, a neuro-symbolic pipeline that decouples semantic scene understanding from physical constraint satisfaction. First, a large language model extracts a typed scene graph from the problem text. A deterministic solver then converts this graph into a Planar Straight-Line Graph (PSLG), encoding force balance, optical paths, and field topologies as exact geometric primitives. Finally, a fine-tuned Qwen-VL model implements a visually grounded propose-verify loop to iteratively correct any constraint violations. Evaluated on a benchmark of 1,449 problems spanning mechanics, optics, and electromagnetism, PhyDrawGen significantly outperforms GPT-5-image, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 3 Pro, demonstrating robust physical accuracy even on unusual-object problems.

</details>


### [223] [Physically Viable World Models: A Case for Query-Conditioned Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30542)
*Adam J. Thorpe,Stepan Tretiakov,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Su Ann Low,Xingjian Li,Hassan Iqbal,Neel P. Bhatt,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，具身AI的世界模型必须具有物理可行性——能够回答干预性查询，而不仅仅是预测观测结果。现有模型可能产生视觉合理但物理错误的推演，因为不同物理系统可能外观相同但干预结果不同。作者提出，世界模型应识别出回答特定查询所需的最简物理抽象，由模块化组件（环境表示、状态参数估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询响应）构成，并通过自主编排器动态组装。过渡模型可采用解析、模拟、学习或混合形式，但必须保留决定干预结果的结构。正确抽象不是最详细的模型，而是保留查询相关区分的最简模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测预测型世界模型虽然能生成视觉合理的未来预测，但常常产生物理错误的推演，导致推荐不可行动作、错误预测交互结果或认证不安全行为。这一问题本质上是结构性的——不同的物理系统可能外观相同但在干预下产生不同结果，现有模型无法捕捉这种差异。

Method: 提出物理可行的世界模型构建原则：模型应包含模块化组件（环境表示、潜状态/参数估计、动作规范、干预动力学、查询级响应），由自主编排器根据查询识别相关物理抽象，动态组装兼容的学习型和结构化组件。过渡模型可以是解析、模拟、学习或混合形式，但必须保留决定干预结果的结构。

Result: 通过控制基准实验（固定可见场景但改变潜在物理参数）暴露现有模型的问题，并展示了所提方法在对现有系统失败的查询上能给出正确答案。该方法使模型可解释、组件可验证、输出可审计。

Conclusion: 正确的抽象不是最详细的世界模型，而是保留与查询相关的区分的最简模型。这为设计新的世界模型和测试现有模型提供了可行性准则，并概述了编排器如何动态组装和适配物理可行的模型用于规划、控制和验证。

Abstract: World models for embodied AI must be physically viable: constructed to answer intervention queries by representing the physical structure governing action outcomes, rather than merely predicting future observations. Existing observation-predictive world models can produce visually plausible but physically wrong rollouts. This failure is structural; distinct physical systems can look identical yet diverge under intervention. We expose this problem with controlled benchmarks that fix the visible scene while varying latent physics. We show that such models may recommend infeasible actions, mispredict interaction outcomes, or certify unsafe behavior. We argue that embodied AI requires world models that identify the simplest physical abstraction sufficient to answer an intervention query. Such a model comprises modular components, including environment representation, latent state and parameter estimation, action specification, interventional dynamics, and query-level response. An autonomous orchestrator should identify the relevant abstraction and compose compatible learned and structured components per query. When closed-form physics is unavailable, uncertain, or costly, the transition model may be analytic, simulated, learned, or hybrid, but it must preserve the structure that determines interventional outcomes. This decomposition makes the model interpretable, its components verifiable, and its outputs auditable against the query. It also provides a design principle for new world models and a feasibility test for existing ones: the right abstraction is not the most detailed model of the world, but the simplest model that preserves the distinctions relevant to the query. We demonstrate this approach on queries that existing systems fail to answer correctly, and outline how an orchestrator can dynamically assemble and adapt physically viable models for planning, control, and verification.

</details>


### [224] [Transforming and Encoding FTS for SAT Solving: What Helps, What Hurts (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30563)
*João Filipe,Álvaro Torralba,Gregor Behnke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将因子化任务编码为SAT问题，提出了多种将因子化转移关系转换为命题逻辑的编码策略，并分析了并行性利用和任务转换对SAT规划器性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化任务作为一种比STRIPS和SAS+更紧凑的规划表示形式，支持析取前提、条件效应和天使非确定性，但目前仅局限于启发式搜索方法，缺乏基于SAT的规划方法研究。

Method: 提出多种将因子化转移关系编码为命题逻辑的策略，并分析不同层次的并行性利用方式及常见任务转换对SAT规划性能的影响。

Result: 给出了因子化任务到SAT编码的具体方案，并评估了并行性和任务转换对求解性能的作用。

Conclusion: 因子化任务可以有效地编码为SAT问题，通过合理的编码策略和并行性利用，基于SAT的规划器能够处理因子化任务。

Abstract: Factored tasks are a classical planning representation that extends SAS+ with limited forms of disjunctive preconditions, conditional effects, and angelic nondeterminism. This allows for a more compact representation of tasks than traditional formalisms such as STRIPS or SAS+, and supports a wide range of task transformations. However, existing planning approaches for factored tasks have been limited to heuristic search methods.
  In this work, we investigate how to encode factored tasks in SAT. We propose several ways to encode the tasks, focusing on different strategies for translating the factored transition relation into propositional logic. We also analyze how to exploit parallelism at various levels in this setting and study the impact of common task transformations on the performance of SAT-based planners.

</details>


### [225] [Procedural Generation of First Person Shooter Maps using Map-Elites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30570)
*Simone de Donato,Pier Luca Lanzi,Daniele Loiacono*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文應用MAP-Elites算法設計FPS遊戲地圖，提出了兩種新地圖表示方法（Point-Line和Spatial-Layout），相比傳統表示方法能生成更多樣化且更高質量的地圖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 現有FPS地圖生成方法多樣性不足，且缺乏對地圖拓撲特性和湧現特性的有效表徵，需要更適合的表示方法和特徵來引導質量多樣性算法的照明過程。

Method: 使用MAP-Elites with Sliding Boundaries (MESB)算法，引入Point-Line和Spatial-Layout兩種新地圖表示法，基於地圖拓撲屬性和湧現屬性定義系列度量指標，通過深度分析選擇最適合的特徵來引導MAP-Elites的照明過程。
